for a single other student that was caught in the rain.

"You're gonna be late" indeed... The dude hadn't extended his so-called 'kindness' to anyone
else.



"Wh-Why...did you do that...?" The girl, presumably Takamaki, asked. Akira looked to her
and could immediately see the tension draining from her body.

Akira thought about making a joke, saying that he was securing his guide to Shujin, but he
felt that this girl wanted comfort, not a one-liner.

"I could see how scared you were from space," he replied. "I'm not going to pry, but
Kamoshida makes you uncomfortable, right?" Akira stated. Not asked.

Takamaki paused, opening and closing her jaw, like she was trying to find the words. So
many emotions could be seen in her bright blue eyes. Eventually though, she managed to say
something.

"Didn't...didn't I tell you that you could get hurt if you involved yourself in my problems?"

Akira was silent for a second and then took to leaning against the wall. That slip-up was
telling. That thing causing Ann grief? It had to be her connection to Kamoshida.

Akira didn't live on the streets for seven years to assume that she was failing Kamoshida's
class. It had to be something else.

"Yeah, you did," Akira sighed. "But I could have never forgiven myself if I let you get in that
car with him. Call me stupid if you want, but getting involved in other people's problems is
sorta why I'm here in the first-"

"DAMN IT, KAMOSHIDA!"

Akira and Takamaki quickly turned to see a dude with bright, yellow hair sprinting towards
them. He was also wearing a Shujin blazer and pants but underneath he was wearing a casual
yellow shirt with some obnoxious logo on it.

The boy started to slow down, coming to a halt in front of the duo. He put his hand on his
knees and crouched down, looking like he'd just run a marathon.

"Grr... 'Eff me..." He muttered to himself between pants. Sweat ran from his forehead and
dripped down his nose, or maybe it was rain? Akira couldn't tell. It wasn't hard to discern that
he was in pain though. Guy must've pushed himself.

"You...alright, dude?" Akira asked, patting his shoulders. He was soaked through, likely from
a rain and sweat combo. "Y-Yeah, give me a s-sec." The guy replied between breaths.

"What lit you up by the seat of your pants, Sakamoto?" Takamaki asked. She seemed to be a
bit more emotionally stable now, that was good.

"I saw Kamoshida talking to you guys," Sakamoto replied. "He probably tried to get you into
his car, right?" He asked.

Akira nodded his head. "Yeah, dude gave me all kinds of bad vibes."



"Did he?" Takamaki turned to him in surprise. She had been present that entire conversation
and to her, Kamoshida played the part of an average teacher perfectly. It was only behind
closed doors that he showed who he truly was. But this guy saw through it in one exchange?

Sakamoto managed to stand up straight after his sprint and pocketed his hands with a
frustrated grimace.

Was it Akira, or was the guy putting more weight on his left leg than his right...?

"Of course...of course he'd try to get her into his car. That's just what a pervy teacher like him
would do..." The boy muttered.

"Does he do that with everyone or just with you, Takamaki?" Akira looked to the girl. Then
he remembered that he promised that he wouldn't pry and held up his hands. "Ah! Forget I
asked. Sorry..." The last thing that he wanted to do was make her upset.

Takamaki seemed to silently accept his apology, thank god.

"Are you kidding me? You go to Shujin, so you obviously know what an asshole Kamoshida
is, right? He does whatever he feels like. Acts all high and mighty like he's the king of a
castle. It's bullshit!" Sakamoto snapped, kicking the pavement and causing a small spray of
water to follow his foot.

"Just transferred." Akira quickly replied. He gave no more detail than that. They didn't need
it. "I didn't know who he was until five minutes ago, but he does give off that kind of
attitude."

"Oh, so you're new...yeah, makes sense that you don't know him then..." The blond scoffed.
"But you still stopped him, right? Good on you, man!" Sakamoto said, giving Akira a small
smile.

Takamaki crossed her arms. "I-I didn't need his help. I could have said "no" and gone on my
way!" She told Sakamato. Akira believed that hiding a "bullshit" in a cough was in poor taste.

"Anyway, what's your name? It'd be nice to know someone who can also see through
Kamoshida's crap." Sakamoto asked, holding out his hand. Akira looked at it for a second
before grasping it. "Akira Kurusu. What's yours?" He asked.

"Ryuji Sakamoto!" The punkish teen answered with a grin. He then looked over to the girl
and pointed to her. "That's Ann Takamaki."

"Hey, I don't need you to introduce me!" Ann scoffed. She looked to Akira and shakily held
up her own hand. "A-Ann Takamaki. Um...thank you for, you know... helping me out.

Akira smiled at her and gripped her outstretched hand. "You're welcome." He replied. It
wasn't as cheery as he was hoping it to be, probably because ultimately, nothing had been
solved and they were about to head into a school that had a teacher who was also a total
creep.



Ryuji then held up his hand. "Rain's clearing up. We should get to school while it's eased off."
He commented.

"Hey, looks like your teru teru bōzu worked!" Ann chimed.

"Oh, I don't actually have any teru teru bōzu. I was just sorta talking out my ass..." Akira
admitted awkwardly.

"Teru teru bōzu? Ain't that the shit that you hang up to prevent bad weather? You sent
Kamoshida packing with that?"

"It came out of left field, caught him off guard" Akira replied. "Kamoshida could spend his
time trying to challenge my beliefs; convince me they don't work, or he could just move on
with his day. I don't think anybody would want to be part of that conversation..."

"Well then, I guess that your imaginary teru teru bōzu worked..." Ann giggled. It was a sign
that she was feeling better, and Akira would take it. He could see the humour in it.

"C'mon, this way's quicker," Ryuji told the two of them as he walked forward and gestured to
an alleyway down the road. "If there's another spill coming, we should at least be at school
when it happens."

"Yeah, good point..." Ann muttered as she followed after Ryuji. She looked back to Akira.
"You're coming too, right?" She asked.

"Yeah. Wait up!" Akira replied, jogging after the pair of blondes.

Truthfully, Akira didn't know if this was a good idea. He didn't know if this interaction with
these two was a good idea. If it could lead anywhere, there was the big question of whether it
should, given his circumstances.

But, being smiled at. Being appreciated...it was nice to get that from people his age for once.

Well, whatever happened happened. It was time for his first day at Shujin.

Had his focus been on anything other than the backs of the two blondes, he probably would
have noticed the splash of magenta that followed his footsteps...

Chapter End Notes

Who found the Shinada reference?



Chapter 3: In the King's Palm
Chapter Summary

The school is not a school, and Akira shows a bunch of knights that swords are
overrated.

There is also a cat.

Chapter Notes
See the end of the chapter for notes

"What in all the fucks...?"

Akira would be the first to admit that during his orientation yesterday, he wasn't really paying
attention to the school. The layout, atmosphere and structure of the building, he had zoned
out during nearly all of it.

But however out of it he was, he would have to be blind and deaf not to notice the fact that
there was a GODDAMN MEDIEVAL CASTLE where the school was supposed to be,
complete with a moat, drawbridge and ominous pink, swirling skies.

If this was the school then he was Feather Hawk!

But the sign out front said 'Shujin Academy...'

This...this was complete nonsense. This place looked like it belonged in a fantasy book, not
modern-day Tokyo!

How on earth had this happened? Castles this prominent don't just appear out of thin air...

...

...

Okay, most castles didn't appear out of thin air. There was that one in Osaka that he read up
on a few years back. But the concept of a castle suddenly appearing out of nowhere was
bullshit then and it was bullshit now.

He spared a glance towards both Ann and Ryuji, who seemed just as stunned at the sight
before them as he was.

"You're both... seeing this, right?" He asked the pair, turning to them with eyes wide behind
his glasses.



To his relief, both of them nodded. "Uh... yeah, I think so," Ryuji answered slowly, a
perplexed look on his face.

"This can't be real..." Ann muttered, her eyes full of confusion. "I've gone this way loads of
times! It's supposed to lead right to school...why the hell is a castle here!?" She cried.

"You're...both sure that we didn't go the wrong way?" Akira asked. It was a weak question,
but how else could something like this be explained?

"Yeah...I'm positive." Ryuji replied as he dug into his pocket for his phone. "Hold on, lemme
just...huh?" The blonde kid looked at his phone screen for a second.

"What's wrong?" Ann asked.

"I was gonna open my Maps app so I could show you guys, but my phone's got no cell
service." He told her, turning his phone around to show the pair that he was on zero bars.

Ann took a quick look and then reached for her own phone. It took a second for her to affirm
that hers was in the same state. "Mine too."

And Akira...

"What is 'cell service?'"

Ann and Ryuji turned to look at him blankly, as if he had asked if wood floats in water.

"Are you for real, dude?" Ryuji asked.

"Hey, I literally just got this phone yesterday. I don't have all the ins and out of this thing
down yet." Akira exclaimed defensively.

"Just...just let me have a look," Ryuji muttered as he came up to Akira and looked at his
screen. "Yeah. You don't have any service either." He commented.

"And that's...bad?" Akira asked.

"Means you're off the grid. Can't make or receive calls, use the internet, or anything." Ryuji
said. The blonde kid then took a few steps towards the castle and looked up and down the full
scope. "Dammit, what the hell's going on here?"

"Ooh, I don't know..." Ann murmured nervously. Akira and Ryuji looked back to see her all
antsy. "...but it's giving me the creeps. I-I wanna go back the way we came!" She demanded,
pointing towards the alleyway they came from.

"But the sign, it says Shujin Academy, doesn't it? This has to be the school." Ryuji retorted.

"Yeah, and the sky is pink, there's a drawbridge, and there's nobody around when people
should be arriving by now. This clearly isn't normal, Sakamoto!" Akira replied.



"Yeah...but..." Ryuji ruffled his short, blond hair and then stomped the ground. "Gah! This is
too confusing! Look, I'm just gonna go in and see. You two can go back and take the long
way if you want, but this can't be a real castle, I know it." Ryuji said. He then started walking
towards the castle's drawbridge with a firm expression on his face.

"Sakamoto!" Ann cried out to him, but Ryuji continued to walk, ignoring her.

"Dude, come on! This could be trespassing for all we know!" Akira added. He had to admit,
the guy had major balls to start walking up to a creepy-ass castle, but were his instincts shot?
Something was obviously wrong here!

Once it became apparent that Ryuji wasn't turning around, Akira looked at Ann with a serious
expression. "I'll go grab him before he gets himself into trouble. Takamaki, you should head
back. We'll be right behind you." He told her.

Ann stared at him for a few moments, and then to the alleyway. All she needed to do was
leave and get this creepy experience out of her mind...

And yet, something kept her from immediately following Akira's instructions.

"But..." She wrung her hands together. "What if...what if Kamoshida-sensei comes back? If
you're not there, then he'll realise that the whole thing was a trick, and he'll be even more
insistent." She told him with a worried face. It seemed that she chose to be a little more
honest with him. Or maybe she had realised that hiding the truth of the matter was pointless.

Akira looked at her for a few seconds before sighing and nodding his head. "Good point. Do
you wanna stay here then? I'll get Sakamoto and we'll be back in no time."

"Y-Yeah. That works." Ann nodded her head. "Please be careful."

"Yeah, got it. You too. If something looks... weird or dangerous, then the alleyway is right
there." Akira gestured to it. "Only in an emergency, of course."

"Right."

With that established, Akira turned and ran towards the castle and then over the drawbridge.
At the end of it, he arrived at the pair of big wooden doors, one of which were pushed open.
So Ryuji had already gone inside.

Akira took a moment to process how surreal it was that he was about to enter a damn castle
on his first day of school. Just in and out, no big deal...

Akira stepped through the opening and found himself in what looked to be a lavish main hall,
exactly what you'd expect from a regal place. Pillars and torches, chandeliers, standing
torches and indoor balconies. It definitely looked like a place of nobility.

And smack dab in the middle of the hall was Ryuji.

Talking to a knight.



Not someone who was dressed up as a knight for a performance or something, but an actual
knight wielding a longsword and a massive kite shield. And its face was covered by some
kind of green opera mask.

This knight gave Akira all kinds of weird vibes, how could it not? It was actually the same
kind of sensation that he had felt last night...with Igor and the twins...

Just what was going on?

"Dayum, that costume's insane. It almost looks like real armour! Whatever club helped put
that together really outdid themselves!" Ryuji told the knight with an almost frightening level
of casualness.

Was...was he serious?

"Come on, say something. The school's a castle now, right? How long did it take to set up?
What's the occasion?"

Oh god. He was...

The sound of clanking alerted Akira to the arrival of more knights identical to the first. They
strode up to Ryuji who was looking visibly uncomfortable.

"H-Hey! W-Wait, time out!" He held up his hands. "What's goin' on!? Is this... actually real?"
He asked in disbelief. The blonde looked around to try and find a break in the formation to
run, but they had him surrounded.

One of them struck him struck in the back with a shield, sending Ryuji to fall to the ground in
pain. That was when Akira decided that he had watched long enough.

"Hey!" He shouted, stepping into the room. "Back off from him. Right now." He warned. It
achieved his goal of putting all eyes on him, including Ryuji's. "K-Kurusu...?" He grunted in
pain.

"ThErE'S AnOtHeR InTrUdEr. CaPtUrE HiM." One of the knights ordered. The voice was
unnatural, like it was distorted or something.

One of the knights stepped forward, metal boots clanking while sheathing its sword. It started
walking towards Akira.

"I don't know much about the code of chivalry, but I'm pretty sure it doesn't involve ganging
on someone who's unarmed. You're just punks in suits of armour." Akira snarled at them,
subtly getting into a fighting stance.

"YoU WiLl nOt sPeAk tHaT WaY To tHe kNiGhTs oF ThE GrEaTeSt oRdEr tHaT HaS EvEr
aNd wIlL EvEr eXiSt. On yOuR KnEeS, pEaSaNt!" The approaching knight ordered, its hand
reaching out for Akira.

Akira waited.



The hand grew closer.

He breathed in and out.

His fist tightened.

"I said...down on your-!"

CRASH!

"G-GAAAAAAAAAAH!"

The knight went flying. Blown several metres back by something too fast to see. It landed
right in front of the group of knights and Ryuji, coming to a stop with a shriek of metal, full
ass over teakettle. Both the boy and the knights were trying to process what just happened.

The knight had been in front of Akira one second and now it was lying motionless on the
floor in the next...with a massive dent in the armour.

"Wh-What the fu-!?" Ryuji gasped as he looked up to Akira, who was staring at the
remaining knights with his fist raised.

"No way..." He muttered. That random kid...did this?

In unison, the knights broke formation around Ryuji and directed all their attention towards
the foe before them. It seemed they already decided who the more dangerous one was
between the blond kid and the man who had sent one of them flying with a single punch.

"Coming up to me like that, it was like he was begging to be Tiger Dropped," Akira
chuckled. God, it was such an amazing technique, one of his favourites. "Now, what was it
your friend said...? Down on my knees?" He asked with a smug expression.

"If you want me to kneel..." He grinned as he tensed all his muscles and a shimmer of yellow
coated his body. "Then MAKE ME KNEEL!"

Ryuji didn't know about the knights, but looking at Akira right now, he was getting tingles up
and down his spine.

The knights held their swords aloft and charged at Akira, who faced them with his fists
clenched tightly at his sides. It was going to feel good to punch something again...

The first knight lunged at Akira with his sword, but Akira spun out of the way, delivering a
powerful elbow to the knight's chest plate, causing it to crumple slightly. The knight stumbled
backward, but his two companions were quick to close in on Akira.

They swung their swords in unison, but Akira rolled out of the way before popping up behind
the knight and kicking its less armoured tendons. With a grunt, the knight fell to its knees,
giving Akira the chance to roundhouse kick the sucker in the head. The knight crashed to the
ground and didn't get back up.



The second knight advanced on Akira almost immediately after, swinging its sword in a wide
arc. But in a stunning display of perception, Akira quickly moved his hands and caught the
sword between them. The befuddled knight had all of a second to react before Akira spartan
kicked it in the sternum. With a grunt, the knight fell backwards, his sword falling from its
grasp, and remaining in Akira's.

Akira flipped the sword in his grip with a hum. It definitely felt real. They were definitely
trying to kill him. He found himself slipping into a ko-gasumi stance. It may not have been a
perfect stance for a longsword, but it was the only thing that he remembered from his old
school's kendo students.

The first knight seemed to get its second wind and held up its own sword, circling Akira, who
eyed it warily.

The knight lunged forward, thrusting his sword towards Akira's chest. Akira met the attack
with a shower of sparks, his eyes glaring into holes of the soulless green mask.

A reflection on his opponent's armour had Akira quickly disengage as the other knight
rejoined the fight.

"ReTuRn mY SwOrD, aNd i'lL MaKe yOuR DeAtH SwIfT!" The second knight warned as he
thumped its shield on the ground.

Akira grinned. "Whatever you say..." And tossed the sword above their heads. The knight
held out his arm to catch it, only to realise that it was a trap. With crazy speed, Akira rushed
into a perfect striking zone!

"Essence of Uppercut Crushing!"

Akira let his fist fly, nailing the stunned knight right in the centre of its mask with such force
that it went flying back like the first guy, before lying still.

And Akira wasn't done, as he caught the still airborne sword by the hilt and slashed at the
final knight. It tried to bring its sword up in defence, but it was too slow.

With a shriek of metal on metal, the chest plate of the final knight was cut open horizontally,
throwing the knight back before it too fell to the ground, unmoving.

Akira sighed. The battle was over.

He slowly came out Beast Style after confirming that none of the knights were getting back
up. He still didn't know what the situation was with these knights, so he tried to remain
nonlethal.

As he would soon find out, he needn't have bothered.

With a horrid hissing noise, the assaulting knights were engulfed in thick black smoke,
almost like they were on fire. And just like fire, they were consumed by it in an instant, as if
their armour and whatever lay beneath was made of flash paper. Nothing was left behind



"The hell...?" Akira gasped as he looked between each of the knights. All of them vanished in
the exact same manner.

"Huh!? A-Ack! Oh god, I breathed that in...!" Ryuji gasped in disgust as the knight in front of
him turned into black smoke before also vanishing.

Decided that the matter of the vanishing knights could be put aside (as difficult as it was to
do so.) Akira looked at Ryuji before jogging up to him. "Hey, you alright, man?" He asked.
"That guy hit you pretty hard."

Ryuji looked at Akira for a second before he started to push himself up off the ground.
"Urgh...yeah, I should be fine." He replied, brushing off his pants. "More importantly
though...the hell was that, man?"

"F-Fighting...?" Akira replied, suddenly feeling like he'd been put on the spot.

Ryuji hung his head in disbelief. "Uh yeah, of course it was fighting, but you don't get into a
fistfight with four actual, literal knights and win. You put dents in 'em, man. Like, don't your
hands hurt?" Ryuji asked, almost frantically.

Akira opened and closed his hands and then shook his head. "Nah, I punched metal stuff for
training all the time back home. Eventually, you get to a point where it doesn't hurt anymore."
Akira answered.

"Holy shit... You're some kinda badass, aren't you...?" Ryuji replied in amazement. Akira then
proceeded to frown and cross his arms.

"What I am is some kind of pissed. Takamaki and I told you to come back, but you didn't. If I
wasn't here then god knows what would have happened to you." Akira glared at the now
guilty-looking boy."

"U-Umm...sorry, Kurusu." Ryuji replied. "Things were just too weird and I wanted to know
what was goin' on. I didn't think I'd run into this kind of trouble..." He added.

"I don't think that anybody would expect this kind of trouble," Akira replied. "Still, it was
stupid."

"Yeah, I got it. L-Look, can we get outta here now? I don't know what the 'eff is going on
here, but this place definitely ain't Shujin." Ryuji replied.

Akira smirked. "Door's right there."

Ryuji stood up fully and made his way to the entrance with Akira. "But for real though, you
fighting those knights has to be the coolest thing that I've ever seen. You were brilliant, man!"

Akira admittedly had to hold back a smile. That lone statement had confirmed in his mind
that, as strange as it was to fight against medieval knights, his skills had actually managed to
help someone other than himself. If only that night had ended similarly...

"Come to think of it, Sakamoto..."



"Hm?"

"Well, I rescued you from a castle, right?"

"Uh, yeah."

"And people who are rescued from castles are usually princesses, right?"

"Well yeah but...hey, wait a minute...!"

Akira grinned. "All hail Princess Ryuji! May her reign be long and prosperous!"

The squawk of indignation that Ryuji gave would become one of Akira's fondest memories...

As they arrived at the door, Ryuji sputtering to try and retain what little dignity he had left,
Akira suddenly heard something coming from outside. It sounded like voices.

"Like, I know I messed up, but calling me princess is a low blow, dude. I mean, I have my-"

"Dude, shut up for a sec, there's something outside." Akira hissed. The blond quickly shut up
and then huddled up against the massive doors. It was clear that he was going to be a bit more
receptive to Akira's instructions.

The two boys crouched low and focused their ears to the outside.

"H-Hey...you're holding on too tight! It hurts!"

"StOp sQuIrMiNg, PrInCeSs! We'lL GeT YoU BaCk tO HiS MaJeStY'S SiDe iN No tImE!"


"I told you, I'm not a princess! Is this a movie set or something? Am I being pranked!?

"It iS StRaNgE ThOuGh, I CoUlD HaVe sWoRn tHaT We wErE PuRsUiNg aN InTrUdEr..."

"Aahh! I-If you don't let me go right now, I'm calling the cops!"

That feminine voice begging to be let go was unmistakably Ann's, and there were also the
distorted voices of the knights.

Akira and Ryuji slowly peeked out from behind the door and surveyed the situation. As the
voices indicated, Ann was being dragged towards the entrance to the castle, a knight on both
her arms. She was kicking and flailing in sheer panic, yet could not break away.

"Dammit, I told her to run if things got bad..." Akira grunted.

"They probably took her by surprise. Tell me you wouldn't stop and stare if a pair of knights
came up to you..." Ryuji whispered back.

"Point taken. Still, we need to save her!" Akira insisted.

"They're coming our way. We could ambush 'em." Ryuji replied. Akira looked at him in
surprise. "Wait, you can fight too?" He asked.



"W-Well, no...but I could give one of them a good shove, and then you can take them both
out. You're uh, good for that, right?" The blond questioned.

Akira grinned as he started to get into position behind the door. "Is grass green? Is water
wet?" He told the boy confidently.

Ryuji smirked right back and dug the balls of his feet into the ground, ready to strike.

"Careful, dude."

"You too, Princess Ryuji~"

Before Ryuji could retort, the pair of knights stepped through the doors, with Ann in tow.
Both boys stayed silent as they watch the twintailed blonde kick and scream in their grip.
They were completely ignored. Good.

He looked to Ryuji and made a gesture to move on the unaware knights. Ryuji nodded back.
As a pair, they silently advanced on the group, their targets locked.

And in an instant, they engaged.

"Hrrragh!" Ryuji screamed as he charged into the knight on the right. If there was any doubt
that Kurusu was an absolute badass when taking care of these guys, it was assuaged in that
moment, because Ryuji felt like he had just shoulder-charged a brick wall. Still, it got the job
done as the knight tumbled to the ground and its grip on Ann fell away.

On the other side, Akira once again aimed for the weak point at the back of their knees and
kicked them harshly. The knight fell to the ground with a surprised gasp, giving Akira the
chance he needed to grip the top of the knight's helmet and smash its face into the ground
hard enough to leave cracks on the floor. Obviously, that grip fell from Ann's arm too.

Those both happened in about two seconds.

"Ah! G-Guys!" Ann cried in surprise and relief. As soon as her arms were free, she scurried
to the boys' backs, her blue eyes filled with frightened tears.

She was joined by Ryuji once the other knight started to recover. "Kurusu, go for it!" Ryuji
bellowed as he looked to the frizzy-haired teen.

"GrR...! YoU TwO! i tHoUgHt a sQuAd wAs aLrEaDy sEnT To dEaL WiTh yOu. WhErE Is
iT!?" The knight snarled.

"Oh, those guys? They couldn't catch a bee in an apiary. I crushed them." Akira said with a
low voice.

"ImPoSsIbLe, YoU'Re jUsT A..." The knight suddenly noticed the still body of his
comrade. "...pEaSaNt...?"

"Am I now? Then you shouldn't have any problem taking me down." Akira challenged with a
smug expression. The declaration was permeated with a palpable confidence that caused the



knight to falter.

"NgH...D-DoN'T TaKe uS LiGhTlY! yOu aRe nOtHiNg bUt-GaH!" The knight was cut off as
Akira sprinted up to it and punted its kite shield out of his grip and into the air.

"Essence of..."

Akira leapt into the air, grabbed the shield and swung it down in too of the stunned knight
with tremendous force.

"...Self Destruction!"

The knight didn't even get the chance to fight back...

"Are you okay, Takamaki? You're not hurt, are you?" Akira asked, once the threat had been
neutralised and they stood alone in the main hall.

Ann rubbed her arm. "Th-They were pretty rough. B-But I think I'm okay." She muttered.
The girl was clearly trying hard to stay calm despite all of the questions that were
undoubtedly springing to her mind. The knights that Akira had defeated had vanished as if
they were ghosts. What was up with that? And why were they calling her 'Princess'?

And Akira. Watching him was like watching an action movie. He took down those knights in
seconds. She did not expect that from someone she had met no more than five minutes ago on
the side of the street.

"Alright. So, if we're all in one piece, I vote we leave here, like right now. Not only is it
dangerous, but we're probably going to be late if we spend any more time here." Akira
commented.

Ryuji shook his head in disbelief. "Dude, we're in a goddamn castle, being attacked by
knights that turn into smoke when you beat 'em, and you're worried about getting to school
late?"

"If you were in my shoes, you'd be worried too," Akira said, his voice taking on a grim tone
that made Ryuji feel slightly uncomfortable for asking.

"U-Um, I'm all for getting the hell out of here. I don't wanna see this place ever again..." Ann
piped up. That was something that the two boys could agree on.

"Yeah, I hear ya...we should get outta here before any more knights show up. God, they're so
freaking creepy..." Ryuji paused for a second before he looked to Ann. "And, hey. I'm...sorry,
Takamaki. If I didn't insist on stayin' then we could've avoided all of this."

Ann looked at Ryuji in surprise for a moment before huffing. "We'll get out of here for now,
and then I'll decide to forgive you later. If it wasn't for-"

"-If it wasn't for Kurusu, we'd be screwed, I know..." Ryuji sighed. "Let's just get outta here
before something even crazier happens."



Akira looked to the door and waved the two to follow. "Come on, if we head through the
alley, we should..."

Akira froze. It was subtle, but he could definitely feel some killing intent being thrown at
him.

"Hey...did this room just get colder...?" Ann whispered.

"Something... doesn't feel right..." Ryuji added, his eyes glancing around the room.

Akira's instincts were screaming at him that something was about to happen. Screaming at
him to keep his guard raised.

Something was coming, but from where...?

WHOOOOSH!

"DOWN!" Akira screamed as he turned on the spot and entered the Beast stance. Akira's
order was so sudden and so powerful that Ann and Ryuji were acting on it out of an almost
primal reflex.

Akira spotted a single projectile heading straight for them, wreathed in flames like a
miniature meteor. It was fast, almost too fast.

Almost.

He tightened his muscles and focused his mind, waiting for the perfect moment to strike.

As the projectile drew closer, Akira's instincts took over, and with a mighty roar, he swung
his fist across his chest, channelling all his strength into a single blow. There was a deafening
crack as his fist connected with the object, and for a split second, it seemed like time stood
still.

Then, with a rush of air that blasted through the room, the projectile was sent hurtling
towards the far wall, crashing into it with enough force to leave spiderwebs cracks in its
place.

Akira shook his hand a little. That had stung a little bit, almost like he deflected a small
cannonball, but it was nothing that he couldn't handle.

Plus, doing that made him feel like a Dracken Orb character.

Fuck yeah!

Ann and Ryuji peeked up from the floor and stared at Akira in awe, their mouths hanging
open in disbelief. "Whoa...did you see that?" Ryuji breathed.

"It was kinda hard not to..." Ann whispered right back. God, she could barely move her
mouth...!



Akira then glanced at where he sent the projectile packing. Just what the hell had been shot at
them? Scanning the realm of reasonable options in his head didn't return any likely
candidates. What on earth could it have possibly-?

And then he saw it.

It was a volleyball. A completely normal volleyball that had been shot at them like an
oversized bullet. Who in the fuck decided that volleyballs needed to go fast enough to leave
cracks in walls!?

"Well, well, well...it's not every day that I come across someone who can deflect my spike.
How...infuriating."

Another distorted voice came from above them, but it was obvious that it wasn't the knights
from before. It was different, more sinister, and extremely arrogant.

Akira, Ann and Ryuji looked up at the top of the stairs, only to be met with...

"K-Kamoshida!?" Ryuji cried in alarm.

"Kamoshida!?" Ann gasped.

"...dude, where are your pants?" Akira asked.

The man that stood at the top of the stairwell was, to put it mildly, scantily clad. The only
thing that he was wearing that covered his body properly was a fur-lined robe adorned with
hearts. Other than that, the only other things that we wore were a pair of slippers and pink
underwear, as well as a tiny crown atop his messy hair.

But Akira could not mistake him. The man before them was, without question, the same man
that had offered them a ride just a little while ago. What was happening?

"You would address your king so disrespectfully? It is King Kamoshida! You intrude upon
my castle grounds and don't even bother to address me by my proper title? All of you are in
deep shit!"

Kamoshida gave a single wave of his hand, causing the entrance doors to slam shut behind
the trio. Ryuji turned on the spot in shock and dashed over to the door. He grabbed the handle
and frantically yanked at it, but the door wouldn't open. "Wh-What the hell!? Let us the eff
outta here, Kamoshida!" Ryuji demanded, turning to the man, glaring at him with hatred.

"Oh Sakamoto. You just don't seem to learn your lessons. You stand up to me, naively
believe that I can't get away with whatever the hell I want and now you speak with such
disrespect? I'll have you executed!" Kamoshida snarled.

"The hell you talking about, King No-Pants?" Akira asked lowly. There was genuine ire in
the man's voice. He meant everything that he said.

"Hm?" Kamoshida broke his attention from Ryuji and fixed his gaze on Akira, staring at him
with baleful golden eyes. Akira didn't flinch.



"And you. Assaulting my knights, forgoing the respect I deserve... I'll have you both
executed! But, I think I'd like to watch you scream. Perhaps I'll hang you up in the torture
chamber for a few months before I take your head..." He said with an evil grin. "There's
something about the way you stare that really pisses me off. I should gouge out your eyes...
Yes, that will make me feel better." He said.

Then he looked to Ann. "But you, Takamaki... Hmmm, I knew it was only a matter of time
before you delivered yourself to me. You clearly can't stay away, can't you?" He told her
with a perverted smile.

Ann, who was clearly horrified by being come onto by a somehow even worse version of the
teacher, proceeded to hide behind Akira.

"I-I don't want anything to do with you! You're the one who...the one who..." Ann trailed off,
looking down at the ground shamefully.

"Oh, you don't have to say anything Takamaki. You may be stubborn, but you'll come
around... You won't have a choice. Sooner or later, you'll belong to me. After all, you want
her to be able to follow her dreams, right?"

Akira noticed the light slowly dim from her eyes, and she whispered something too quiet for
him to hear. He turned back to Kamoshida and moved to block his view of the girl.

"You're blackmailing her with something, aren't you? Trying to force a relationship, with a
minor with whatever it is." He grunted, clenching his fists at the fury that started to bubble up
within them.

Kamoshida scoffed. "I don't have to answer to you, you pathetic little slime. I can do
whatever the hell I want. After all, I'm King."

He then whipped his robe outwards, giving everyone too much visibility of his near
nakedness. "Unfortunately, I don't have time to deal with you right now. I'm quite busy, so
you'll be going to the dungeons until I'm ready for you..."

Kamoshida snapped his fingers, causing many explosions of black sludge to surround the
teens. To their shock, every one of them gave rise to another abnormal knight, until there
were about twenty in total.

"Wh-Whoa, shit! Guys, come on, we gotta run!" Ryuji cried. He panicked and looked around
for an exit but saw a knight stationed at each one.

"Don't bother. I've had this room completely covered. Nothing goes in or out unless I want
it to. And you," Kamoshida pointed at Akira. "You can't possibly defeat all of these knights,
so if you want to grovel for forgiveness, now is the time."

Akira glared at him and assumed his Beast Style once again. Truthfully, being able to defeat
the knights was irrelevant, he knew that he could do it. What really made things difficult was
the fact that he'd have to protect both Ryuji and Ann too.



Still, he'd be damned if he didn't try.

"If one of us is going to be grovelling at the end of this, it's going to be you, King No-Pants!"

Kamoshida gave Akira a cruel grin and then walked away, heading deeper into the castle.

The knights drew their swords and slowly advanced on the trio. With nowhere to run, both
Ann and Ryuji huddled up to Akira with terror clear on their faces. "K-Kurusu...you can
handle this many, right?" Ann asked, her voice quivering.

"I should...but not while I'm protecting you guys..." Akira admitted. "I'll try to clear a path
through them. Once I do, you two make a break for it, at least keep out of their reach.
Kamoshida wants us alive, to torture me, to creep on Ann and to execute Ryuji."

Their faces grew grim at the summarization.

"But I promise you two this. No matter what happens, I won't let you die. I'll save you." He
told them. The knights grew closer and closer. And Akira grit his teeth.

"COME ON!" He shouted as he jumped into the mass of knights.

Akira's Beast Style was a force to be reckoned with. With each strike, the knights fell one by
one, their weapons no match for his strength and agility. But he knew that time was not on his
side. He had to defeat them quickly if he wanted any chance of saving Ryuji and Ann and
getting the hell out of here.

The room was filled with the sound of crunching metal and fallen swords. Even with a whole
gang of knights, he battled them with flawless application and brutal strikes. He broke bones
(if these knights even had any,) slashed swords, and slammed their faces into each other.
Knights fell one after the other. Yet, for as hard as he fought, he couldn't clear a path for the
pair. Not one that stayed open long enough for them both to dash through, anyway

It was when he had a pair of knights' faces smashed into the floor that he saw it - out of the
corner of his eye, he caught a glimpse of Ryuji and Ann being dragged away towards one of
the room's exits.

His heart sank as he watched them disappear from view, their screams of terror ringing down
the halls.

"Ah! Crap! Get offa me!"

"No! No no no! Kurusu! Please help!"

"Dammit! Hold on!" Akira shouted, standing up, only to be met with more of the knights.
The pair's screams were growing fainter by the second...

No, don't panic. They would be kept alive.

He got up from his most recent victims and looked at the remaining knights. Eight in total.



"I'm sorry, knights, this isn't personal or anything, but I've gotta finish this quick..."

Akira took a step back and readied his stance. One that radiated frightening power.

"Who's first? Plenty of Tiger Drops to go around!"

Yeah, Akira had pretty much given up on getting to school on time at this point.

"Shit shit shit shit shit shit!" Akira cursed over and over as he sprinted down the halls of this
crazy castle. The walls turned to blurs and stairs were taken four steps at a time. Obviously,
he didn't have a clue where he was going, but he'd seen the knights take Ryuji and Ann this
way. It was either pursue the only lead that he had, or leave the castle, call the authorities, and
try and convince them of everything that had happened.

He was already on probation, he didn't need to be thrown into a cell again for drug usage...

No, he'd have to save those two alone.

Akira continued to run, noticing that the gaudy blurs were starting to change into dreary,
oppressive blurs.

And...was that the sound of water that he heard?

As Akira continued forward, the sound of rushing water grew louder and louder until he
finally emerged into another part of this castle, and what he saw before him was so absurd
that it stopped him in his tracks.

It was some kind of large chamber, lined with muddy-red stones illuminated only by flaming
torches, with parts of it partitioned off by thick cell doors. The rushing water sound that he
heard came from an aqueduct in the floor, running all the way down the length of this place.

Was aqueduct the right word? Maybe it was more appropriate to call it a channel?

From the ceiling, thick chains dangled down above the water. Some were simple chains,
others were manacles to hang someone by their arms, and there was even a massive cage, big
enough to put a person in... This just kept getting more and more creepy.

Akira scanned the chamber, looking for any signs of the others, but all he saw were other
macabre 'decorations' that were strewn all over this area. Rusty trays for carrying food on,
chests filled with whips, nails, even barbed wire. Akira felt sick to his stomach just looking at
it. This was the perfect example of a torture dungeon.

There were cells lining the walls of this place, but a quick peek in them revealed that they
were either empty or the doors were outright open, which meant either they were waiting for
prisoners...or the much more horrifying possibility that-

"YEEEAAAAAARRRGH!"



Akira immediately flinched, chills running up and down his body like electricity. What was
happening?

He had just sprinted into a horror movie, that's what was happening!

The only thing that kept him from turning tail and sprinting back the way he came was the
knowledge that Ryuji and Ann had to be down here somewhere. But the only way to find
them was to head deeper in... God he hoped that it would only be more knights that he'd have
to deal with.

Akira gathered his courage and took a single step forward, but then heard the sound of heavy
clanking.

Speak of the devil.

Akira quickly darted to one of the empty cells and hid himself inside. Until he knew for sure
where his schoolmates were, he shouldn't draw attention to himself...

He peeked out from within the call and spotted a group of knights returning from beyond the
barred partition. They probably weren't on patrol, but Akira didn't want to be lax and find
himself in more trouble.

He pressed himself against the wall and covered his mouth. The clanking steadily got
louder...closer, until they were right outside the cell door!

Thankfully, that's all it was, and the squad continued on their route, marching down the same
passage that Akira came from.

Akira waited for ten seconds, then thirty, then sixty, before finally emerging from his hiding
place. That could have been really dicey.

After taking one more glance, Akira shot out of the cell and ran up to the barred partition.
The door was unlocked. Either the guards were lazy or they were confident no one would
escape. Whatever, it worked out for him.

He continued further into the dungeon the sound of his footsteps echoing against the stone
walls. He hoped that he was going the right way. The only plan that he had was to follow the
water.

And over the sound of that rushing water... he heard something.

"...Hey! Let us the hell outta here, assholes!"

CLANG!

"Ack! Shit...!"

There was no doubt about it. That was Ryuji!



With relief now blossoming in his heart, Akira sprinted after the voice. His feet pounded the
ground beneath him as he stared forward resolutely. They'd better be safe...

He eventually came upon a cell to his right, the final cell in this long-ass hallway. Inside, he
saw two figures that he was all too happy to recognise.

Ryuji was leaning up against the cell bars with a frustrated expression on his face, while Ann
was curled up on the wooden cot in the back of the cell, looking close to a panic attack.

Akira skidded to a stop in front of the cell. "Sakamoto! Takamaki!" he exclaimed. "Thank
goodness I found you!"

Both teens bolted upright in surprise. They glanced to the front of the cell, to see their mutual
ally.

Ryuji looked up at him with a grin, his frustration bleeding away to show sheer relief etched
all over his face. "Dude! Holy crap, I thought they got you too! You alright?" The blonde
asked, gripping the bars.

"I should be asking you guys that. I thought that screaming was yours for a second there."
Akira replied, causing Ryuji to grimace. "Yeah...man this place is soooo effed up..."

Akira sighed and pressed his forehead against the bars. "Sorry, Sakamoto, I said that I'd keep
you guys safe, but..."

"Don't worry about it, man. They had us surrounded. What matters is that you're here no-
waah!?"

Ryuji was suddenly pushed aside as Ann got off the cot and rushed up to the bars. "Oh thank
God! You came to save us, right Kurusu?" She asked desperately.

Akira couldn't help but smirk. "Of course. After all, I couldn't leave the vulnerable Princess
Ryuji in the clutches of such a wicked place~"

"Wha-! Dude!" Ryuji spluttered. "S-She's also locked up! Why ain't she the princess!?" He
pointed to Ann, who couldn't help but giggle, despite the grave nature of the situation.

Akira smiled at his attempt at levity before looking at the cell doors, his face falling into a
frown. "In all seriousness though, this thing looks pretty thick. It's gonna be difficult to get
through it if we don't have the key." Akira muttered to himself.

"Wait, you don't have a key?" Ann asked, her face twisting back into nervousness. "B-But if
you don't have a key, how can we get out...?"

"You can just break this door down with a single kick, right, just like with the knights! After
all, your strength's the real deal. I tried punching one of those knights when he took us away,
and I couldn't make a dent!" Ryuji said, with eyes filled with enthusiasm. "My knuckles still
hurt, even."



"I mean...I could break it down, but it'll take more than one kick to do that. A lot more."
Akira replied as he thumbed his chin. "If I go that route, there's no guarantee that I can bust
that thing open before a guard comes to check out the commotion. I gotta get you guys outta
there before any knights show up. If they show up in the same numbers as before, I might
never get you out." Akira admitted.

"So...wh-what should we do then?" Ann asked.

Akira sighed. "Just...just give me a second to think." He groaned. He directed his sight behind
the duo and into the cell. "No chance of being a secret passage like in the movies?" He said
with an awkward chuckle.

Ryuji shook her head. "We already searched this place. Nothing here but barrels, that bed
thingy, and those chains on the wall. We thought about hiding in the barrels to make 'em think
we escaped, y'know, so they'd let their guard down. But it's too obvious. It's the only place to
hide in here... " Ryuji replied.

"That so...?" Akira mumbled as he managed to squeeze his head through the bars and take a
closer look at the cell himself. It was just as Ryuji said. There was next to nothing that looked
like it could assist in escaping.

No, there had to be a way.

He scanned the room from top to bottom, with Ann and Ryuji looking at him anxiously...

Hm?

Oh, that could work...!

"Sakamoto, Takamaki I have an idea, but I'll need your help," Akira told them, with nothing
but seriousness in his voice. Both the blondes stared at him attentively.

"Sakamoto. Those shackles on the wall. They look old. See if you can separate that open one
from the chain. Takamaki, set those barrels next to the bed. Get one of their lids off and leave
it slightly ajar."

"Huh? What are we doing exactly?" Ryuji asked.

"Oh, yeah, I should probably tell you what I've got..." Akira said as he retracted his head and
grinned.

"Alright, here's what we're going to do..."

"Pah! You're useless, every single one of you!" A certain king snarled as he stormed
through the dungeon with an entourage of knights. "How could you all fail to capture a
worthless peasant? There were twenty of you, for shit's sake! Now he's scurrying around
my castle like a rat, and it's all your fault. If he isn't found within the hour, I'll be taking
YOUR head!"



"F-FoRgIvE OuR InCoMpEtEnCe, KiNg kAmOsHiDa! I AsSuRe yOu tHaT We'lL
ApPrEhEnD ThAt sTrEeT TrAsH AnD GiVe hIm hIs dUe pUnIsHmEnT!" One of the knights
replied nervously.

"Hmpf...well, on the bright side, I have Sakamoto and Takamaki to play with..." He said, a
nefarious grin stretching across his face. He was coming up to their cell now. Maybe he'd
break Sakamoto's other leg, maybe he'd give Takamaki a good fondling after tearing off her
clothes. Or perhaps both. Yeah. That sounded good. He wanted to leave this cell with both
their eyes devoid of hope.

With a flutter of his robe, he stepped in front of the cell...only to find it barren. Takamaki and
Sakamoto weren't inside.

"What!? How did they...!?" Kamoshida began to ask, before his brain caught up with him.
There was no way that they could have escaped; the door was still locked.

They had to be hiding in the room somewhere. And by somewhere, he meant literally the
only place to hide. Those barrels weren't fooling anyone.

One of the knights fiddled with the keys before opening the door, allowing entry to
Kamoshida and the knights. Without needing a command, the knights stood in front of the
barrels, ready to punish the foolish prisoners. They were as dumb as rocks if they thought that
this could fool them. Hell, one of them was even slightly open. Utter amateurs.

Kamoshida decided to have a little fun.

"Gee! I WONDER where they could have gone. They're probably not inside these
BARRELS. I guess we'll be on our way now." Kamoshida then obnoxiously stomped his
feet in front of the barrels, as if he was walking away. He smirked as he imagined the
quivering pair inside the barrels.

He then nodded to his knights, two of them placing their feet on the barrels. And with a wave
of his hand, he put an end to their little game.

CRUNCH...!

Or...it should have been. There was the crunch as the barrels shattered, but no fearful screams
or timid whimpers.

And more importantly, there was nobody inside them at all.

"W-Wait, what...?"

Creak...

Click...

The door!



Kamoshida and the knights all turned at once to see the cell door had been shut, with them
inside. And not only that, a metal clasp was being affixed to the top of the door, securing the
edge of it to the static bars. They had been locked in!

And worst of all, on the other side of the bars, there stood Takamaki, Sakamoto and that
damned glasses brat that had been screwing up his plans left, right and center!

"Bastards! Release me immediately!" Kamoshida snarled, rushing up to the bars and
gripping them tightly. He rattled the door, but the clasp held tight, only allowing a little
wiggle room.

He didn't understand! How did they escape? Where had the third teen come from?

"You know, it's funny..." Ryuji said, looking at Kamoshida all cocky. "You'd think a
volleyball player would be more used to lookin' up."

Looking up...?

The realisation hit the king like a bolt of lightning.

"The rafters...!?" He gasped, with his gaze drawing to the top of the cell where there were
wooden beams lining the walls. They must've climbed up the bars of their cell and waited in
the rafters until they opened the door, distracted by the barrels, then they dropped down and
escaped, locking them in with a detached manacle.

"Ugh, I feel like I'm gonna throw up, that could have gone sooooo bad." Ann groaned.

"It almost did. If I didn't catch you guys, we probably would have been noticed." Akira
added.

"Still feels weird that you did that..." Ryuji added.

"Where the fuck did you come from, you shitstain!?" Kamoshida snarled, practically
spitting venom.

"Oh, I was hiding in the canal. Popped up behind you the second you went in." Akira replied
with a smug expression.

"Uh guys, we should go. I don't want to stick around to see how long that holds them." Ann
quickly commented.

"Yeah, sounds good!" Ryuji replied as he set off down the dreary path. "C'mon, we're getting
out of here!" He announced.

"Oh, one sec!" Akira replied, before walking up to the bars, Kamoshida glaring at him like a
crazed animal. "Hey, King No-Pants?"

"IT'S KING KAMOSHIDA, YOU INSOLENT-!"



Akira reached into the cell, grabbed the man by his robes, and pulled him into the cell bars
with a loud clang. Then he did it a second time, and then once more for good measure, the
man crying out in pain each time.

"Aaaargh! You l-little..." Kamoshida cursed before Akira shoved him to the
floor. "ffffffffffuuuuuuuuuck!"

One of the knights caught the man, but that did nothing to soothe his skull.

"It's "mud" as far as I'm concerned." Akira snarled, before turning around and running after
the other two. Part of him hoped that they wouldn't be able to get out, but he knew that they'd
probably only have a few minutes head start.

Better skedaddle.

Unfortunately, the way that they had come from had been blocked off. The partition had been
locked this time, which meant that they needed to find a way around it.

The route that the three teens stumbled upon involved using cages to jump across the
aqueduct, and finding a broken cell wall that they could crawl through to reach another area.

"We-We don't actually have to crawl through there...?"

"Takamaki, it's our only way out."

"But my ti-tights might get torn..."

"..."

"..."

"Welp, sorry, Sakamoto, it looks like our road ends here. We'll be apprehended any second
now, but Takamaki's tights are worth more than our lives, apparently."

"Sh-Shut up! I realised how stupid it was when I said it... Let's just go...!" Ann blurted, red-
faced.

It wasn't long after scurrying through the tunnel that they found themselves before their next
obstacle, a raised drawbridge, with a disgusting stone bust of Kamoshida on its side. Talk
about pretentious!

"Shit! How're we supposed to lower this thing? I'm not seeing any buttons." Ryuji exclaimed.

Ann stared at it for a second before then looking at Akira. "Could you try punching it?"

Akira gave an incredulous snort. "Takamaki, I can't punch all of our problems away. Only
most of them." He snarked. "But take a look." He pointed across the channel. "This is a...ah, I
read about this somewhere...well, this bridge has two halves on either side and they both



meet in the middle. Even if I could knock down one side, the other is still stuck." He
explained.

"So...we've got no choice but to find the mechanism..." Ryuji grumbled. "But there's nothing
here!"

"Yes, there is, and I could show it to you if you release me!" A high-pitched, almost childlike
voice suddenly rang out.

The trio froze on the spot, and their heads turned to inspect a lone cell at the end of the
corridor. Was someone in there?

That's when they noticed something small, black and white grasping at the bars. And that was
small with a capital S. This thing barely came up to their kneecaps. And it looked like...

"W-Waaaah! A m-monster cat?!" Ann yelped, immediately backing away from the cage.

"Holy shit!" Ryuji cursed "Why is this place so freaking weird!?"

Indeed, this...creature seemed to be some kind of bipedal feline, but with a bulbous, oval-like
head, and even had a tiny utility belt around its waist.

Akira just silently stared at the thing.

"Wh-What!? I'm not- L-Look, the key to the door is right there...!" The feline-like creature
pointed to a single key that hung on a hook right next to his cell.

"What?! You can not be serious! You look even weirder than the knights. What if we open
the door and you start tryin' to rip out faces off?" Ryuji asked, his brow furrowing in
suspicion.

"Or what if you're just trying to trick us by pretending to be a prisoner? You could be the
most dangerous thing we've seen so far!" Ann added.

"Rip your faces off!? Pretend to be a prisoner? I'd never do stuff like that. I'm a gentleman...I-
I promise." The creature replied. It looked downtrodden for a second before looking up.
"Look, believe whatever you want, but I do know the way out of here. You wanna escape? I
can bring you to an exit! I know a surefire way to get around the patrols. As long as we keep
our heads down, you'll be out of here in no time!"

"A-An exit?" Ann asked.

Ryuji scowled. "I don't trust it," he muttered. "It could lead us into a trap, or, like I said, rip
off our faces."

"I woooooon't!"

Ann, on the other hand, was more curious than wary. Mainly because this thing offered a way
out. "But it's saying it can help us," she said. "And it seems harmless enough."



"Harmless?" Ryuji scoffed. "Takamaki, literally nothing in this place that we've seen is
harmless!"

Jingle

"Look, we don't have time for this," Ann said, stepping forward. "We need to find a way out
of here, and this might be our only chance. Plus, even if it doesn't have the best of intentions,
have you forgotten who we have with us? He could probably neuter it before it tried
anything."

"N-N-Neuter!?" The feline squeaked.

Ryuji crossed his arms. "Dammit, we don't have time to discuss this. Those knights will be on
our asses any second!"

Click

"Yeah, so we might as well have someone here who knows where to go!" Ann argued.

"But like I said, he could be lyin' about it. Kurusu, what do you-?"

Creeeeeeeeeak...

A squeak of rusty metal silenced the duo's argument as the cell door swung open. The
creature proceeded to trot its way out of the cell. With a relieved grin, it stretched its stubby
arms and legs...

"Ah, freedom tastes so-MROW!?"

Only to be engulfed by a surprise embrace from the frizzy-haired kid that released him.

Akira had been silent up until now, but now he was muttering something that sounded like
"Holy crap, I can't believe it...!" And "He's even more cuddly than I imagined...!"

"H-Hey! Frizzy Hair! L-Lemme go! This is embarrassing...! Put me-Ah, no! Not between my
e-eeearrrss...! M-Mroooow~ Purrrrr~ Meeewww~"

The feline tried to break free from Akira's grip, but it was a futile effort, and when Akira
started stroking his head, then it completely devolved into adorable purring and meowing,
with its disconcerted expression melting into one of blissful relaxation.

"Uh, dude? The hell!?" Ryuji cried as both he and Ann watched Akira act completely
different to how he had done so far. He was... snuggling with this monster cat like it was
some kind of plushie! What kind of badass snuggled with plushies?

Ann thought the scene was weird but also kind of cute.

Akira broke attention from his curry captive and looked up at the two blondes. "Guys! Can
you believe this!?" He asked, holding Morgana up. "He's a real-life Kitty Kat!" He
announced excitedly.



"...Eh?" Came the confused responses of the other two.

The 'Kitty Kat' seemed to snap out of the trance while Akira wasn't petting him. His white,
furry cheeks slowly began to pinken and he flailed in Akira's arms.

"I am NOT a cat! I am Morgana! Now quit showing me off and put me DOWN!" He
demanded. The...not-cat, or so he claimed, seemed to be genuinely upset, so Akira was
brought out of his euphoric state and slowly lowered him to the ground.

"Uh, you do know that cats are...real, right? They're not mythical creatures like unicorns or
kappas." Ryuji commented with a deadpan look. "And "Kitty Kat?" Really?"

"Huh? Yeah, I know that." Akira replied, watching as Morgana embarrassedly brushed
himself off. "I don't think that Morgana's an actual cat; I was talking about the line of toys
that you can only get from UFO Catchers. You know, the Kitty Kats. They come in calico,
blue, red, tiger, and American Short Hair, but I've never seen a black and white one..." Akira
explained.

"T-Toys?!" Morgana cried in indignation.

"UFO Catcher..." Ryuji muttered. "Now that you mention it..."

"Yeah, he totally looks like one of those Kitty Kats!" Ann announced with a bright smile
"Suddenly, he doesn't seem so suspicious!" She added merrily.

"Wa-Wait! Now you think I'm a toy? I'm not! I'm a human, just like you guys!" Morgana
retorted with probably the last thing that any of them expected."

"Human?" Akira asked. "But you look just like..."

"I know, I know, I'll explain later! But we don't have time! The shadows'll be on us any
minute!" Morgana insisted.

"Shadows...?" Akira whispered to himself. What the hell were shadows?

"Aw crap, we got distracted!" Ryuji hissed. Now that they listened, there was the distinct
sound of metal boots, rushing to their location.

"Morgana, you said you knew the way out, right? Please, take care of us." Ann begged,
interlocking her fingers desperately.

Morgana looked to Ann.

He blinked twice.

His heart began to beat rapidly.

His ears and tail perked up, pointing straight at the ceiling.

Ann's beauty instantly entranced him, and her hopeful gaze immediately left him giddy.



It was love at first sight.

"N-No problem, my Lady, just leave it to me!" Morgana squeaked. The sudden formality
made Ann chuckle.

"You can call me Ann if you'd like."

"Lady Ann..." Morgana purred, smiling brightly as he repeated that name over and over again
in his head. "A wonderful name for a wonderful woman~"

"Monamona! We need to go, now!" Ryuji cried. The knights were almost on them!

The urgency in his voice seemed to snap Morgana out of his trance. "Oh! Right, right!
There's a lever in the jaw of that statue over there. If you pull it, the drawbridge will lower.
And it's Morgana!" He told them.

"Sakamoto!" Akira quickly called upon Ryuji to confirm Morgana's words. The blond rushed
over to the bust and pulled its jaw down. Then there was the chittering of unseen machinery
which gently lowered the two bridge halves onto place, allowing them passage.

"Hell yeah! Let's book it!" Ryuji cried as he dashed over the bridge.

Morgana grinned. Surely this gesture of goodwill would win him some points with Ann.
Perhaps she'd give him a hug, or maybe even a kiss...!

"Thanks, Morgana!" Ann chirped as she too started to make her way over the bridge.

"Whuh!? W-Wait, Lady Ann...!" Morgana called out, but Ann was already gone. The not-cat
seemingly deflated and a depressed aura clung around him.

Yeah, that would probably have been too easy...

"You good, Morgana? You look kinda down..." Akira asked, being the only one to notice the
cat's depressed state.

"Yeah...let's...let's just go..." He replied, slinking toward the bridge.

"Well...if you're feeling upset, I could always give you another cuddle?" Akira offered.

"I don't want that kind of attention from you!"

"But we can't just leave them here!"

Their grand sprint lasted no more than half a minute before they came across something that
made them stop in their tracks.

Other people.



No knights, nothing outrageously perverted like what Kamoshida had going on, no. There
were people down here, just like them...students.

"And I said we don't have time! We could be captured at any second. Saving these guys are
pointless.

"Pointless?" Akira questioned. Morgana, a guy is hanging upside-down getting repeatedly
beaned with a volleyball getting shot out of a cannon while three other guys are running for
their lives on a treadmill with rolling spikes on the end. If we left them to suffer..."

"That's just how this place works!" Morgana cried impatiently. "Can't you see that they're
different from all of you? Helping them does nothing.

"Different from us...?" Akira muttered, leaning into the bars. He watched the exhausted
students sprint on the treadmill, desperately trying to accomplish that Sisyphean task of
tasting even a drop of the water at the end. "Now that you mention it..."

These people didn't feel like the others, like Ann, Ryuji or even Morgana. They felt weird.
Like, the knights weird.

"They're...like the knights?" Akira asked, causing Ryuji to look at him in confusion. "Like the
knights?" He pressed? "The hell are you talking about?

"Yeah, they're like the knights." Morgana quickly replied. "Those people down there, they're
not real. Think of them like...I dunno...like props. Props that can move and speak, but aren't
actually alive." He explained.

"Wait, are you talking about, like, robots or something?" Ryuji asked.

"Also not too far off. Look, there's not enough time for me to explain it all, so let's-"

"Those are the uniforms of the volleyball team..."

Everybody looked to Ann, who had been quiet up until now.

"Every student down there...they're all members of Shujin's volleyball team. That's Taokaka,
there's Daki...something. Yeah, these guys are definitely part of the volleyball team..." Ann's
voice sounded quivery, and her face was pale.

"L-Lady Ann?" Morgana asked.

"Hey...yeah, you're right! Looking at 'em now, all these guys are part of the volleyball team!"
Ryuji gasped in realisation. "But then, why would there be robot versions of 'em in this
backwards ass place?" He asked.

Morgana drew his attention from Ann and focused it towards Ryuji. "I just said that there's no
time to explain. We need to leave!" The cat stated firmly. He then dashed away on his tiny
legs gesturing for the others to follow.

"Hey! Wait up!" Ryuji cried, sprinting after the feline.



Akira took a glance at Ann. She still seemed to be thinking about something that had to do
with the volleyball team. "You gonna be okay?"

"I-I don't know," Ann replied.

"Can you still run?"

"Y-Yeah, I can. Sorry." She apologized. The blonde shook her head as if to distance herself
from those thoughts that had plagued her.

"Let's go."

"Morgana..."

"Y-Yeah?"

"You said that you had a surefire way to escape, which would take us through minimal knight
patrols and be quick as long as we kept our heads low."

"I d-did..."

"So then, why are we currently standing before an entire retinue of goddamn knights in the
only room of the castle where they'd feasibly fit!?"

"D-Don't blame me! The original path I had was way too heavily guarded. I had to
improvise!"

"Improvising by strolling through the main hall?"

"It was...supposed to be sn-sneaking..."

Akira would liken their situation to being thrown into a lion's den with meat hung around
their necks.

Morgana had initially tried to bring them one way, but ten knights had blocked their path, and
Morgana had insisted on using another exit. Which just so happened to only be viable to them
by crossing the main hall.

They had barely made it halfway across before shit rocketed fanwards, and countless knights
inundated the hall in explosions of black sludge. Akira didn't bother counting them all, but he
knew that it was somewhere over a hundred.

"Oh god...oh god...!" Ann whimpered. There were so many of them that she had started to
totally freak out. She probably saw that cell again, with a plan that wouldn't work a second
time.

"No...no effin' way..." Ryuji whispered. There was no disguising the quiver in his voice. The
blonde was petrified by the sheer amount of knights.



Even Akira, for all of his strength, wasn't sure if he could take on that many knights at once.
He had his hands full dealing with a fifth of them.

"D-Don't panic! I have one more trick up my sleeve." Morgana whispered as he started
frantically searching in his utility belt. However honeyed his words were, the trembling on
his part made it clear that he was also feeling the pressure.

And then came that horrid laughter. The slapping of slippers off the marble floor. And just
where he had stood before, Kamoshida was staring down at them like they were rotting fruit.

"Wh-What!? We left him locked up!" Ryuji gasped. "How did he get free so fast!? How did
he get here before us?"

To that, nobody had an answer.

"Hahahahahahaha! Such moronic peasants. Did you really think that you could escape
that easily?"

"I'd argue that none of this had been easy-"

"Like the oblivious ants you are..."

"Oh, you're still talking, okay, fuck me, I guess."

"...you paraded through my castle, thinking that you've been staying undetected. Well, I
have news for you..."

His grin grew even more malicious.

"Nothing happens in this castle that I don't know about! Including your desperate bid to
escape." He flicked his hair up. "Like rats on a sinking ship."

"You know, you're acting awfully cocky for a man who got locked up in his own goddamn
dungeon!" Akira retorted.

"Dude! The hell are you doing!?" Ryuji hissed. "You're just gonna piss him off!"

"Well, he's pissing me off." Akira shot back, his glare never breaking from the evil king.

"Oh...? I piss you off, huh?" Kamoshida said with a snarl. "What a bold thing to say to
your king."

Akira grit his teeth. "Damn right, you piss me off. I don't know what the hell is going on,
where we are, or why it's happening, but what I do know is that you've been giving me and
my friends shit ever since we got here, and I'm sick of it!" Akira screamed at the perverted
king.

"My, my, my. Look at you, all fired up," Kamoshida chuckled, clearly amused by Akira's
outburst. "But do you really think you can do anything about it? You're just a speck of



nothing, while I am a king. The ruler of this world. You are no more than a bug to be
crushed under my feet."

Akira clenched his fists, trying to control his anger. All of this conceitedness... If it weren't
for all of these knights, he'd already be up there, ripping him in half.

"A king with a paper crown, no doubt. We've seen what's in the dungeons, what you do to
your subjects or whatever the hell they are. I've seen how you look at Takamaki and
Sakamoto; with arrogance and lust." Akira said, his voice low and steady. "You're no king.
You're just a tyrant and a bully, with, might I add, the worst fashion sense I've ever seen."

"Careful what you say now. Every single word makes me want to increase your torture
tenfold." Kamoshida warned, his hands gripping the banister.

The king's eyes trailed over to Ann and Ryuji, who were standing behind Akira timidly. A
grin returned to his face.

"And you have the audacity to speak of my actions when you've thrown your lot in with
those two? Have you any idea of the things that they're responsible for? How much pain
they've caused?"

Ryuji and Ann froze in place, petrified by what Kamoshida just said. It was clear that they
both knew what he was talking about.

"H-He can't mean..." Ryuji whispered.

"But I was...!" Ann squeaked anxiously.

Akira looked behind him to see that the pair was noticeably shaken by what the king said.
"Everything alright?" He asked them.

Ryuji suddenly pushed past Akira and pointed this finger at Kamoshida in accusation. "Don't
you effin' dare call what happened my fault. You were the one responsible for that!'

"But it was your fault, Sakamoto. So many dreams thrown to the wind, all because you
couldn't control yourself..." Kamoshida shook his head as if he were pitying the blonde.

"You...you...!"

"You know I'm right..." Kamoshida said.

"No...no! You were the one who put us through all of those exercises with no breaks! You
didn't give a shit about running, so you abused us until we broke. You got our old coach
fired! You purposely provoked me so I-!" Ryuji stopped himself and took shuddery breaths.
His emotions were getting the better of him.

And no one else was put through that? Even now, you're acting as if you're the lone victim.
Shameful!



Akira frowned as he was forced to listen. It was obvious in retrospect, but Ryuji seemed to
have a reason to be so hateful towards Kamoshida. He leaned back to Ann.

"Takamaki, what happened between Ryuji and Kamoshida?" He asked.

"H-Huh? Oh, um, well...the official story is that Ka-Kamoshida was the coach for the running
team at one point, the one that Sakamoto was on. But then one day, Sakamoto attempted to
assault him, and in self-defence...Kamoshida...he..." The girl trailed off.

"What did Kamoshida do to him?" Akira asked seriously.

"He..."

"...broke his leg."

Akira swore that he felt his heart stop. He broke his leg? In self-defence? In what universe
would going that far constitute as self-defence?

A fully grown man; an athlete, had broken a teenager's leg. On a team of runners. That would
be crippling.

"Blame me all you like, Sakamoto. Doesn't make it any less true that because of your
selfishness, so many of your peers whose dreams it was to run have been crushed!"

Ryuji's eyes lost their accusatory fire, and the boy fell to his knees. Kamoshida may have
been a son of a bitch, but none of what he said was incorrect. He had allowed himself to be
goaded. Allowed himself to fall into Kamoshida's trap. He'd been stupid.

"That useless running team was Shujin's pride and joy. Without it, the school may have
just faded away into athletic obscurity. Enter Suguru Kamoshida, the hero of Shujin
Academy. Without my volleyball team, the school may have lost its athletic draw altogether.
I took them to nationals! I won prestige! While you, Sakamoto, you just pissed it all away.
Now, you'd be lucky to even graduate!" Kamoshida spoke with an insufferable sneer.

With the blond boy verbally beaten down, Kamoshida looked back at Akira with cruel eyes.

"Now, answer me honestly. Is a selfish, moronic, insignificant shitstain of a person like
him even worth trying to save?" Kamoshida asked.

Now, it was worth noting that Akira hadn't known Ryuji for more than a couple of hours. The
boy was next to a complete stranger to him. So was Shujin Academy. He had no idea of the
influence of the volleyball team, nor the prior influence of the running team. He didn't know
a lot of things. Did Ryuji's problems really have to be his own? They weren't even on a first-
name basis.

To be frank, beyond getting everyone back, he had no obligation to this entire situation. This
whole thing about teachers with king complexes, Ryuji's acts of violence, and medieval
castles in modern-day Tokyo were matters that he should have nothing to do with. He was
on probation, and here he was, picking fights with weird monarchs, and running incredibly
late for his first day of school.



There had to be more to the situation, but the bottom line was that if he wanted to stay out of
trouble and not bring any attention to both himself or Morning Glory, he needed to keep his
head down. He needed to be the kind of person who looked away from people in need.

A bystander.

A coward.

But...

There was an undeniable distraught on Ryuji's face that Akira would never be able to ignore.

That Kazuma Kiryu would never ignore.

"If I may summarise, your Majesty..." Akira began in as mocking a tone as he could. "You
coached the running team beyond harshly, you provoked Sakamoto into attacking you in
order to claim self-defense and dish out an injury that no one reasonably should, you got the
old coach fired, and had the pride of Shujin at the time abolished, all so you could prance
around with your own favoured sport. You murdered the lead actor of the play so you could
get the role!" Akira accused. "If I'm feeling any animosity towards anyone, it's not Sakamoto
it's you!"

Kamoshida scowled in response while Ryuji looked at Akira in shock.

"And the most disgusting thing about it is how goddamn proud you are. You're making it out
like you're a hero, but you're just a prick who thinks that crippling children, torturing them,
makes you hot shit." Akira shuffled over to a nearby floor candelabra. "It just makes you a
normal piece of shit."

He hefted the bulky thing in his hands, playing with the weight. "So, sorry if this whole idea
of causing infighting between us didn't work out..."

Like fire, golden cinders of power started to roll off Akira's body.

"In fact, it's only assured me that you deserve this!"

With that cry, Akira heaved the golden candelabra at Kamoshida with all of the technique and
ferocity of a hammer throw.

"Wh-What the-!?" Kamoshida gasped.

There was a clank, and a distorted groan as a lone knight took position in front of the king
just before the strike. It didn't get its shield up in time though, so it was met with a painful
impact that left a dent in its breastplate. Both it and the candelabra fell to the ground.

Akira clicked his tongue. Damn knight legion.

"Wh-What the hell!? What was that?!" Morgana looked up from his...pile of scrap that he
was playing with and stared at Akira incredulously. Normal humans weren't supposed to have
that kind of strength!



"I could tell you, but I'd prefer you to keep working on whatever's supposed to be saving our
lives right now," Akira replied, not breaking eye contact with Kamoshida at all.

"Oh, right!" Morgana squeaked as he quickly returned to what he was doing.

He didn't understand though, the only thing that warranted that kind of power within
somebody was a Persona. But he couldn't sense one inside of him, not a presence like his
own. So...did this guy just get lucky?

"So...you'll defy me to the very end, will you? That's a poor decision you shitty little
insect."

"I've been defying you all day, why stop now?" Akira retorted.

Kamoshida turned up his head and began to pace back and forth. "Hmpf, I hate to admit it,
but you've been a massive thorn in my side ever since you got here. You've made a fool of
me, brought my knights to their knees...and that look in your eyes. I hate it. I hate it!"

He held out his hand and growled in a low voice. "My ball."

Immediately, one of the knights procured a nondescript volleyball and placed it into
Kamoshida's hands.

"It's clear that I can't afford to be lenient with you anymore. I'm done giving you chances
to wriggle free. I'm going to end you all with one shot! You, Sakamoto, Takamaki, and that
cat!"

There was a massive surge of power around Kamoshida, and Akira could immediately tell
that this would be unlike anything that they had yet seen.

The man suddenly jumped high in the air, and let the ball drift from his hand. "Be thankful
that the majesty of this technique will be the last thing that you ever see!"

There was a whoosh, followed by what sounded like a loud rubbery snap as Kamoshida
spiked the ball with terrifying might. Akira readied himself to deflect it once again.

And then...

It split.

There were no other ways to put it. The ball fucking cloned itself and what was once a single
meteoric projectile turned into a hundred-strong barrage!

Akira was certainly not prepared for this!

No, don't panic...! The balls were powerful, but they were easy to read. Keep a cool head.
Rush Style would be able to get him out of this in one piece...

But as he was about to switch, he remembered that this attack wasn't meant for just him.
Ryuji was still knelt in front of them. Ann and Morgana were gaping at the onslaught. None



of them were able to move. This move was beyond overkill for normal people like Ann and
Ryuji and probably Morgana.

If he dodged, then they...

"SHIT!"

Akira grabbed the back of Ryuji's blazer and turned on the spot to throw him back over to the
other two before he even realised what happened.

Then, he ran at the now group of three and crash-tackled them to the ground. No time to be
gentle. He got surprised gasps, meows and grunts, but they'd just have to deal with it.

From there he brought them all together using his body as a shield against the onslaught. And
all he could do after that was...close his eyes and hope that it didn't hurt as bad as he
thought...

"Dude!?"

"Kurusu!"

"Frizzy Hair!"

Then, the volleyballs began to land with tremendous force. On the floor around them, and on
Akira's back. And any wishful thinking that Akira possessed was dashed in that fury of
volleyballs that each hit like a sledgehammer.

He took the strokes to his legs, his head and everywhere in between. The pain was
excruciating, unlike anything that Akira had ever felt before.

He wanted it to stop. He wanted to make a leap to safety but he forced himself to stay in
place. Forced himself to be a stalwart barrier between the attack, and his allies.
