of mental shutdown. He was especially clear on that part, just so there'd be no question as to
how risky this method was. 



When Akira was done, Ann stood there in a stunned quiet, her expression indescribable, as if
her face couldn't decide which emotion to show. 

To think that this whole plan that they had kept her in the dark about involved that terrifying
world that she never wished to return to. And Akira, Ryuji and Morgana were jumping back
into it willingly? Putting themselves in danger. Holding a life in their hands?

It was a burden that she wouldn't wish on anybody. Especially someone like Akira. For a
moment, she even considered speaking up. Telling them that this was too drastic. Too
dangerous. That there had to be another way...

But then she remembered the call she received in the restaurant. Remembered how
Kamoshida callously tried to punish her for standing up to him by replacing her with Shiho.
Remembered how, even on the way home, Shiho hadn't stopped trembling. Remembered how
scared she had been until they could sit on the couch at her place and try to forget the horrible
events with a bowl of chocolates and whatever was on TV. Remembered what would have
happened if Akira had been just one minute late...

She couldn't stop them. Not after all Kamoshida had done. All he would do. Not after they
had resolved to do what no one else would, no matter the consequence.

But still...

She was brought out of her musing as she felt a hand on her shoulder. Akira had reached out
and gave her a reassuring nod.

"Try not to worry, Ann, okay? When this is all over, we can just go back to being regular
students. Shujin will be safe for all the volleyball team. For Suzui. For you." He smiled at her.
A soft, gentle smile that completely contrasted the mighty badass Akira was underneath.
"And when this is all over, we can all go out somewhere nice to eat. You can even bring
Shiho along too. My treat!" He offered. 

"Oh! So you'll treat her, but you won't pay ME back for spending my own money on you..."
Ryuji grumbled from behind Akira, crossing his arms. 

Akira rolled his eyes and glanced to Ryuji. "Stop whining, I'll pay you back within the
hour..." He responded, before pausing and briefly glancing down at Morgana. "I'll be able to
pay him back within the hour, right?" He asked the cat.

"Hmpf, if we do everything right..." Morgana responded.

"See! Nothing to worry about!" Akira announced. It could be argued that there was quite a lot
to worry about.

Ann said nothing. She didn't move an inch as Akira stepped back from her. He chose to take
that as compliance. 

"Alright," he sighed, glancing over to Ryuji. "Let's get to work."

"Right! Let's do it!"



And the blonde girl watched as the pair, plus cat retreated further into the alleyway, away
from prying eyes. She watched as Akira pulled out his phone and navigated to the curious
app. And she watched in astonishment as the trio suddenly blurred into nothingness, as if
they had been erased from existence by an invisible... something. 

And then...she was alone, standing outside of the alley with nothing but a chest heavy with
anxiety. She waited five minutes. And then ten. The student migration had slowed to maybe
one a minute.

The girl then looked down at the ground and pulled her hands out of her jacket pockets.

 

Her fingers were crossed.

 

"I'm sorry, Akira. But I can't...I can't just do nothing."

Ann didn't know what she could do. And her better judgement bade her to reconsider. But she
wouldn't. Ann could tell that if she didn't do this, then she'd regret it forever. Even if it meant
returning to that world. Even if it meant facing up to that even more horrible version of
Kamoshida. Even if she could only provide a single second of assistance, she had to try.

It was irrational, foolish and dangerous above all else, but Ann didn't care. She had to help
put this demon to rest.

She wandered into the alley whilst pulling out her phone, with an ominous red glow coating
her face as soon as she launched the app. She took a deep breath, tried to control her
trembling, closed her eyes and tapped the button. 

"Beginning navigation..."

Ann felt it before she saw it. The oppressive sensation that tingled against her skin. It was a
familiar and entirely unwelcome feeling. It almost felt like she was being consumed by a
virus, which was supposed to be the Palace's attempts to force its influence onto her, if she
remembered Morgana's lecture correctly.

Idly, she wondered if something like UV sunscreen would repel that uneasy sensation, but no,
that would just be stupid.

...right?

She opened her eyes and immediately noticed the muted colours permeating through the
already dismal alley. Glancing up at the sky, she could see it was pink instead of blue, just
like it was the first time she came here. It was unquestionable; she was back in the Metaverse.
Back in this hellish place.



"God, this is so crazy..." Ann whispered to herself. To think she could return to this place on
her own accord after what had happened, all without knowing if she could even help. For a
moment, the rational part of her brain surfaced, told her that this was asinine, that she should
leave the way she came before she ended up getting in the way. But she quickly shut it down.
Buried that decision under the desire to help, to take part in the downfall of the man who had
come within a hair's breadth from raping her friend. To help the person who wished to deliver
her from all of her grief.

In any case, she wasn't going to get anything done by simply remaining in the alleyway. She'd
given the guys a ten minute head start, so hopefully by the time they realised she followed
them, it would be more trouble than it was worth to force her out.

She nodded. "Alright...I just need to stay low and keep quiet." She muttered as she turned
around to face the castle. "As soon as I meet up with the the others, I can—"

"You can do what, Ann?"

 

...crap.

 

"H-Heeeeeyyyy, guys...!" Ann awkwardly greeted. She forced a smile, as if that would do
anything to rectify the situation.

Standing just at the exit to the alleyway was Akira and Ryuji, clad in black three-piece suits
and leather jackets, staring hard at her behind domino and skull masks. Akira had his arms
crossed and looked completely unamused while Ryuji stared at her, jaw agape.

"Dude...what the hell are you doing here!? We told you to stay out of this!" The blond cried
in sheer disbelief.

"I-I...um...it's so-sorta like this–"

"Save it, Ann," Akira spoke in a thorny tone of voice that Ann couldn't help but flinch from.
"I told you to let us handle this. You literally just promised me that you wouldn't get
involved!"

Ann felt her voice immediately leave her, and she couldn't manage a retort. Not that she had
anything to retort with. Another poor move was failing to come up with anything to say when
she'd eventually meet up with the guys. She thought that she'd have time for that!

"What would have happened if we weren't here? What if you ran into more of those knights?
This place is dangerous, Ann! What was I going to tell Suzui if you got killed, huh? Use your
goddamn head!" Akira snarled. It was such a contrast from the kind boy who told her
everything would be alright a mere ten minutes ago. It made Ann feel like the scum of the
earth, even if all she wanted to do was help.



Akira let out an aggravated groan and pinched the bridge of his nose, or the part the mask
covered anyway. He couldn't help getting frustrated considering that this was a tense
situation, and Ann just strolled in here to make their job more difficult.

But before Akira could continue to berate her, they heard Morgana's voice harshly whisper at
them. "All of you get in cover and stay quiet! The guards could be back any second!"

Everyone glanced at the not-cat, who peeked out from behind the wall emblazoned with the
name 'Shujin Academy'. When he ascertained that the coast was clear he waved the others
towards him and mouthed "Hurry! Hurry!"

"Shit..." Ryuji shook his head and dashed over to hide behind the wall next to Morgana.

Ann immediately felt her heart rate spike. She could feel the same kind of dread that ran
through her body the first time when she was here. If it wasn't for Akira grabbing her wrist
and rushing over to the wall with her in tow, she may not have moved at all.

In any case, once they were hidden, Morgana peeked out from behind the wall, pointy ears
twitching as he tried to detect any sounds that indicated the arrival of the Shadows.

"What's the sitch, Mona?" Ryuji asked with a low voice. "We still clear?"

The question went unanswered for a few seconds, and Ryuji was about to ask again before
Morgana pulled himself backwards so fast he nearly tottered over. "Not clear! Not clear!" He
hissed.

Immediately, the other three could hear the sound of shuffling, rapidly clanking metal and
distorted grunting. It was the unmistakable sound of Kamoshida's legion of Shadows. No one
dared move an inch.

Ann was seriously trying not to freak out. The sheer tension of the moment had her holding
her breath for longer than she thought possible. Had the knights already detected them?
Should they run?

The answer to that question was no, as thankfully, the procession of knights didn't come
anywhere close to their hiding spot. Instead, they passed by with an audible haste that
indicated an important task.

Morgana waited for half a minute before he slowly peered around the wall again just to
confirm that no one had spotted them, and that appeared to be the case.

Since watching was apparently fine, Ryuji shuffled over and took a glance around the wall as
well. His curious gaze was soon joined by Akira's and then Ann's.

They saw an entire squadron of Kamoshida's knights moving about. Several of them were
stacking sandbags and another group had thick logs propped up while using chisels and
gouges to form them into large wooden stakes.

Ann's brow quirked with in confusion. "Wh-What are they doing?" She whispered.



Ryuji squinted at the scene, the more intrinsic features of his expression hidden behind his
skull mask. "They've been bringing out sandbags 'n shit ever since we got here. Looks like
they're going to war..."

A noticeable difference to the armoured, mask-wearing abominations was a knight clad in a
complete suit of golden armour with a firey plume atop its helmet. That guy was clearly a
rank or two above the other guys.

A knight captain of some kind?

"hUrRy uP, yOu lAyAbOuTs!" The high-ranking Shadow's distorted voice grated on their
ears. "LoRd kAmOsHiDa hAs eStImAtEd mErE HoUrS BeFoRe oUr fOe aRrIvEs! We
mUsT SeCuRe tHe pErImEtEr!"

Ann looked down at Morgana with a bewildered expression. "They're trying to strengthen
their defenses? Is...is that normal, Morgana?" She questioned.

Morgana thought for a few seconds before he shook his head. "I've been to a few Palaces
already, and aside from an increase in guards, none of them ever made an effort to fortify
themselves like this. It's really bizarre..."

"Not like this whole situation ain't effed up to begin with..." Ryuji muttered. "What do you
think they mean by 'foe'?" He asked.

Morgana took a moment to contemplate the scene before them. "I couldn't say. But if I had to
guess, it's probably a result of the Palace's security level being so high, likely because of the
stunt that this one pulled," he said, thumbing towards Akira.

"I did nothing wrong." The frizzy-haired teen responded curtly.

Ann bit her lip. If there was any part of her that was questioning the direness of this
endeavour, it had now been sufficiently quashed. "S-So, you're saying that we can't infiltrate
the palace?" She questioned

Thoughtfulness danced in Morgana's blue eyes and he rubbed his cheek. "It'll be tricky, but
not impossible." He deduced. "Most of the guards are out fortifying defenses, so the interior
of the Palace itself will be minimally guarded. If we can find an opportunity to slip past the
knights outside..."

Ryuji's face lit up. "Then there'll be less on the inside to keep us from the treasure!"

"So if we can get inside, we'll have an easier time moving around." Ann hummed.

The two boys plus cat glanced at Ann critically and she felt a wave of apprehension. "Wh-
What?" She asked.

"Ann," Akira sighed. "You're not coming with us. You're gonna turn around, go home and let
us deal with this. Like you promised."

"What!? B-But I–"



"I know you want to help, but this isn't a game. Without a Persona, this place is a deathtrap."
Akira explained. Of course, he was an outlier to that statement...

"Th-Then I'll get one! You just gotta get angry, right? I'm angry! I'm so pissed off at
Kamoshida that I want to shove his stupid volleyballs down his throat!" Ann grunted as she
balled up her fists.

"Ann, it's..." Akira shook his head "...it's not that simple. I don't understand shit about getting
a Persona, but it isn't as simple as just getting angry. I'd prefer that you stay safe in the real
world than chance that you'll awaken to one here." He told her.

He stared back at Ann's aggrieved face with unrelenting seriousness. "Plus, if it turns out that
Kamoshida does have a mental shutdown from this, then the less people accountable for
that–"

"I don't care."

"What?"

Ann looked down at the ground, and her clenched fists started to tremble. "Shiho...she was so
afraid. Even when we got home, she couldn't stop shaking. ...Do you know how hard it is to
see your best friend like that? I wanted to help so bad but... It was like before. I-I couldn't do
anything."

The guys went silent while Ann continued to speak lowly.

"I felt helpless. Like nothing. Like nothing I did mattered. All this time, I've done nothing but
watch as you stepped in to save us, time and time again. I couldn't save Shiho any other way
than by being friends with you."

"Ann. That's–"

"And I know it's selfish. But I want to do something. I need to do something. Even if it means
Kamoshida could die, even if it means that I could die. I can't hang back and be...nothing
anymore. I wanna stand on my own two feet."

Akira was silent as he stared at the blonde.

"I'll do whatever you tell me to do in there, and I'll stick to you guys like glue. I'll try to-- no,
I won't get in your way. And even if it hurts, I'll try to get a Persona too. I need to help tear
down this castle, even if it's just a little bit. So please, please let me help you." Ann begged.
She looked to be seconds away from going full dogeza.

Both Morgana and Ryuji were stunned at the declaration, and neither of them seemed to
know what to say. So, both of them deferred to Akira. "So uh...what's the call here?" The
skull-masked boy asked.

Akira turned his eyes up at the dismal sky. To be honest, if that was all Ann had to give, then
he probably wouldn't have been convinced. Ann's safety and her innocence mattered more
than her whims of being an asset ever could.



But they weren't just words. It was the resolve; the courage burning within them that truly
stated her case. Ann was scared, but she still chose to put on a brave face. To finally stand up
against the bastard that was ruining her life. To run no longer.

It was naive, but someone like Akira respected that resolve. To give all she could, even if it
wasn't much. And with such a genuine desire, maybe...

"Haaah," Akira placed a hand against his head. "I must be out of my freaking mind..." He
muttered.

The trio looked at him with surprise. But the lone girl's shocked face quickly flushed with
gratitude.

"You'll do exactly as I say, exactly when I say it. If I tell you to hide, hide. If I tell you to run,
run. If you so much as sneeze without my permission, we're taking you straight home. Do
you understand?"

Ann swallowed and nodded her head. She wasn't going to whine about the strictness when it
was already a miracle that Akira was letting her tag along.

"Ann..." Akira furrowed his brow in warning. Silence wasn’t enough.

"Y-Yes, I understand!" Ann's voice cracked from nerves.

"Well, if he thinks that it's okay..." Ryuji scratched his scalp resignedly. As if this job wasn't
already going to be stressful enough without a Persona-less party member shadowing them.

"Oh I don't," Akira said. "But if she wants to help so bad she'll follow us into this fucked up
reality, then the safest thing we can do is take her with us. I don't trust her to stay out of this if
we returned her right now anyway. She's still got the app, remember?"

Ah, that was a good point. The last thing they needed was Ann wandering in here alone,
which she had almost done not a few minutes ago.

"O-Oh come on! It's not like I want to look for trouble!" Ann exclaimed. But all she got in
return were a set of stares that had her shrinking back.

"Well, like it or not, you've stepped headfirst into it. So you better stay close and do what I
tell you." Akira told her. "And if you put even a toe out of line, you're uninvited to the
outing."

"Wha--? Hey!" Ann whined. That wasn't fair! Going out to eat with friends sounded really
fun...

"Oh! And not just that, I'll ask Shiho what your favourite foods are and I'll order them for
myself. Then I'll send you a picture of every bite I take. Down to the last crumb..."

 

What!?



 

That... that was cruel and unusual punishment! Akira couldn't do that to her! Her chocolate
parfaits with extra whipped cream...

"Look, just don't pull any crazy stunts, and we won't have any problems," Akira concluded.

"Oh, you mean like how you didn't kick down those massive doors and beat a bajillion
knights in like, two seconds the last time we were here?" Ryuji snorted. Akira rolled his eyes
and flipped the blond the bird.

He then dug into his coat and took out something that had Ann's eyes widening.

 

A handgun.

 

"We'll try to protect you, but there's no telling what could happen in a fight. So if you think
that you're in danger, use this. It's only got eight shots, so use it only if you think you have no
other choice." Akira explained.

Ann slowly reached out and wrapped her hand around the grip of the gun. It was true what
they said, guns were heavier than they looked. She hefted it in her grasp a few times, trying
to adjust to its deceptive weight.

Akira stepped back and allowed Ann to get used to the feel of the gun. It wasn't exactly a
foolproof plan, but it was better than nothing. Akira needed that insurance, if for nothing else
than his own peace of mind.

There was a frantic hiss as Morgana made a grab at their attention, and they spun around to
look at the not-cat.

"Guys! There's a break in the patrols! Now's our chance! We'll sneak in through the vents."
Morgana whispered, his tail swishing frantically.

Ryuji sighed and nodded his head. "Right. Now or never." He glanced back at the pair.
"Ready, Joker?" He asked the teen clad in black.

"...Joker?" Ann asked, tilting her head like a confused puppy.

"We'll explain later," Akira answered quickly. "We gotta move. Come on." He waved her to
follow, hands clad in crimson gloves.

Ann swallowed and gripped the pistol tighter. "R-Right!" She squeaked. "Lead on, Joker!"

The first thing that the group learned when they got into their first fight with a Shadow was
just how intricate the nuances of combat got. Shadows came in numerous forms that were



revealed once they snuck up and ripped the masks off of the knights. They had weaknesses
and strengths that could be exploited to gain an edge in battle.

Unfortunately, there was no way to know what they were weak to without just throwing
magic at it. Lightning, curse and wind were all they had to work with right now, and while it
was good to know what kind of Shadow was weak to what, it wasn't sustainable. None of
them had unlimited magic, and if they were careless, they could run out.

The second thing they learned was that Akira was frighteningly good at negotiations...

As soon as Morgana taught them that they could convince Shadows to give up money and
items, Akira rushed up to the two-horned horse they were fighting and gave it a rising kick to
the chin, knocking it onto its back with its legs in the air like a dead fly.

While both the team and the Shadow tried to figure out what the hell had happened, Akira
placed his foot on the horse's belly and grinned down at it cruelly.

"Cash. Now." He demanded from the Shadow roughly.

"Gah! Y-you won't get a--!"

"I won't get a single yen out of you, right? That's what you're gonna say?" Akira added
weight to his foot.

"Gngh...!"

"I'm not in the mood to play games, so here's what's going to happen. You're going to cough
up every last yen you have, or I'll break off both your horns and sell those instead!"

"Wh-what...!?"

"Actually, no. I'll only sell one of them, and the other one I'll give back to you..."

Akira's grin grew even more menacing as he got closer to the horse's face.

"Wanna guess where I'll put it...?"

"Heeeeek! T-take it! Take it all!"

The Shadow immediately folded and despite having hooves, somehow managed to throw a
smattering of coins and bills at Akira.

Akira chuckled and removed his foot. "Pleasure doing business..." He sneered.

In the background, Ryuji, Ann and Morgana were staring at the scene, none of them quite
sure what to say.

"Is uh...is this how negotiations are supposed to work?" Ann asked.



"I feel like I'm watching something totally different to a negotiation..." Ryuji added, his usual
slouch now pulled ramrod straight.

Morgana gave an awkward chuckle as he watched the Shadow flee and Akira grab the
money. "I uh...well if it works..."

"Guys!" Akira called out to them, his prior cruel demeanour swapped with a sunny
disposition with sparkles drifting off him. "He gave us five thousand yen!"

The cat gulped. Defeating enemies in a Palace like this should have yielded only a tenth of
that amount and Akira had squeezed out ten times the yield from just one Shadow.

Yup, he had definitely created a monster.

After Akira divvied the money between all of them, (letting Ryuji keep his share to finally
pay off his debt) the team advanced through the castle, sneaking around corners and taking
the enemy by surprise whenever they could. Each battle ended pretty much the same way:
with Akira standing over their defeated foe and demanding money like a regular punk.

A punk that was very good at squeezing out oodles of cash from his victims.

 

"Don't wanna pay me in cash? Fine, I'll take your teeth as tribute then!"

 

"Your wings look so delicate. I wonder if they'd survive being nailed to the wall..."

 

"You seem like a smart Shadow. Give me your money and maybe I won't be hiding under
your bed later tonight..."

 

Every single Shadow cracked and desperately paid to not have Akira rearrange their
skeletons. Akira might have felt a bit bad, but Morgana had told him that Shadows had deep
pockets which refilled after each encounter. So he could mug to his heart's content with none
of the guilt that came afterwards.

It would be inevitable however that they would eventually run into a Shadow that had
nothing to give, no matter what Akira said.

"Ple-Please! I sw-swear! I don't have a single yen on me! N-Not one!" A whimpering fairy
stammered. The Shadow looked to be on the verge of tears, but that didn't earn her any
sympathy from the one whose shoe she was currently pinned under.



Akira groaned and brought his foot lower, making the Shadow gasp timidly and claw against
the rugged leather. "You're testing my patience," he told her. "And I don't have much of it left.
You better give us something if you don't want me to turn you into a stain on the rug."

"N-No...I swear...!"

The teen frowned. He had tried a bunch of different threats that had usually worked on prior
marks, but the Shadow wasn't budging. Perhaps she truly didn't have anything...

"Joker!" Morgana called. Akira looked at him with a small "Hm?"

The not-cat shook his head. "There's no point wasting time on stubborn Shadows. If she
really had anything, she would have given it by now. Just kill her and we can move on."

"W-Wait!" The fairy Shadow held up a hand in a final attempt to bargain. "I...I don't have
anything...b-but I can join you guys! You seem cool a-and confident, we could t-totes get
along!"

Akira glanced back down at the Shadow, unimpressed. "I'm seconds away from needing to
scrape what's left of you off the bottom of my shoe, and you think we can "get along?" He
asked.

The fairy winced, but she nodded her head frantically. "Y-Yes! I swear-- I'll renounce King
Kamoshida's rule and fight by your side!"

And in that moment, the Shadow's countenance suddenly changed, her face shifting from
terror to realisation, as if she had been struck by some kind of epiphany.

Seeing the shift in expression had Akira frowning in confusion. What was this Shadow's
deal?

Before he could ask that question, the Shadow spoke up in a calm voice. "I remember now.
I...I don't belong to King Kamoshida! I am an existence that drifts about in the sea of
humanity's souls."

"Say what...?" Akira asked. He took a quick glance over at Morgana, but he seemed to be just
as clueless as to what was happening as Akira was.

"My real name...is Pixie. I'm Pixie! From now on, I'll live on inside your heart!" She
declared.

"You'll live on in my...huh!?" Akira asked, completely dumbfounded, but by the time he
looked back, Pixie was already shrouded in a blue light that zipped away from under Akira's
foot. Before he could even attempt to understand what was going on, the light rushed straight
to his face.

"I am thou. Thou art I."

Akira, like the combat extraordinaire who had put countless hours into refining his reflexes
that he was, stood there like a complete moron as the light flew into his face. Or more



specifically, his mask. He felt it warm up as it glowed with some kind of strange power. It felt
oddly pleasant, like a warm flannel on a chilly morning.

It seemed to die down soon enough, and once it had, there was no sign of the Pixie. That
strange business with his mask hadn't seemed to affect him in any way, no injuries or
anything.

With nothing to go on, the group had no choice but to continue forward without finding out
exactly what happened.

That was until they entered their very next fight when Pixie leapt out of Akira's mask instead
of Arsené and everybody lost their shit.

Click...ka-klick...

 

"...so not only are you crazy good at fighting, but you can negotiate with Shadows and turn
them into your Persona, which you can then fight with, and you can hold multiple at once on
top of that! I knew that you were special but this...this is something else!" Morgana rambled
enthusiastically.

"Yeah, I'm gonna go out on a limb here and say that normal rules don't apply to this guy."
Ryuji hummed as he peeked around the corner, keeping an eye out for any guards.

They had taken a wrong turn at one point and hit a dead end, but it wasn't all bad, it had given
Morgana the opportunity to teach Akira another essential art in thievery.

 

Click...ka-klick...

 

Ann had taken to sitting on a sofa right next to Ryuji and glanced at the blond boy. "You
know, I've got to say that it's kind of weird to think that a girl is technically in his head now.
What if she shares girl secrets with him?"

Ryuji looked back to Ann and raised a brow behind his mask. "Girl secrets? Like what?"

Ann huffed in reply and crossed her arms. "Wouldn't you like to know..."

 

Click...ka-klick...

 



"Ooh, maybe he'll want to dress up as a magical girl, and fight the forces of evil with love~"
Ryuji snickered. He put his hands together in a heart shape and put on a high-pitched voice.
"I, the embodiment of light and love will beat you with the power of friendship, hope and my
fists, mwah~!"

And Ann completely lost it. She desperately tried to hold back her laughter since it would
likely give them away to any nearby Shadows. But the thought of Akira doing something like
that was just too funny! She fell sideways on the couch whilst covering her mouth to keep the
giggles in.

Of course, Akira overheard all of this and rolled his eyes. At least they were having fun...?

"No, keep the tension on the pins as you turn, otherwise you'll have to start over!" A
supervising Morgana scolded him.

"Ah, sorry!" Akira whispered as he turned his attention back to the lock he was trying to pick
on this treasure chest. He sucked in a breath and slowly worked on the lock, just like
Morgana had taught him. A feather touch, a feel for the shear-lines, six pins to set in place...

 

Click...ka-klick...

 

"...but seriously, what girl secrets? I wanna know!"

"Pft, I'm not telling you. It's bad enough that he might know...!"

 

...

 

You know, Akira didn't really have any friends back in Okinawa. He had a few acquaintances
at school, but nothing that was worth mentioning. Morning Glory was the closest thing he
had to a social circle.

But listening to the pair of blondes bicker while Morgana coached him in the art of
lockpicking, he would be lying if he said he didn't feel a sense of unity forming, both out of
battle and in it.

It had taken some getting used to, fighting in a team. A part of Akira had, and still was,
willing to fight through this entire labyrinth alone, but Morgana had insisted that fighting in a
team was safer. When in a team, you had someone to watch your back, who could play roles
that you couldn't. Each burden lifted from each member was an essential factor that tipped
the scales in their favour.



It wasn't perfect, of course. They were a bit sloppy when it came to combat, and Akira did
feel that it may be easier to do it himself...but he could see potential. Ryuji, Morgana, and
perhaps even Ann, would go from battle neophytes to capable fighters that he could trust
wholeheartedly in a brawl.

 

...At least in the Metaverse.

 

But for now, he could hold back, let them get a feel for fighting, and give them opportunities
to grow. Wait his turn and then bring the pain.

Of course, that wasn't to say that he just stood there while they were in a fight. Especially
when the enemy attacked him. Honestly, what idiot would just stand there and let the enemy
get a free hit in?

In the recreational area of a Kamuro penitentiary, Prisoner 1119 sat in an uncomfortable
metal chair, holding a worn acoustic guitar, surrounded by the chatter of other inmates either
playing board games or reading, pretty much any menial activity to stave off boredom.

He shifted in the rickety seat. It wasn't ideal, but it was better than sitting on the ground.
Opened on the nearby table was a book titled 'Guitar for Dummies.'

He plucked the strings idly and listened to the melodic twangs in response. He sighed and
skimmed the page he was currently on before putting his fingers on what he thought were the
right frets.

Welp, he had eight years in the clink. He may as well learn how to play an instrument in the
meantime.

He placed his fingers on the C and D strings and strummed out a mangled version of his
favourite song.

"So...come on...let's go...level...up...!"

Ka-klick!

 

The lock finally gave way and Akira breathed a sigh of relief. He had managed to
successfully pick his first lock!

"Yes! You did it, Joker!" Morgana cheered. "Keep that up, and every chest is ours for the
taking!" The cat announced. Then he grinned sheepishly. "...for as long as I've got
lockpicks..." He added.



Akira chuckled and pried open the chest to claim the spoils inside. Whatever it was, it was
sure to go for a bunch of--

 

...huh...

 

Akira reached into the chest and pulled out a weighty object about the size of his palm.

It was a golden bust.

Of Kamoshida.

Ugh...maybe he should bin it...

 

...

 

Eh, money's money.

The scouting of the castle was going well. They met minimal resistance as they made their
ways through the gaudy halls. With most of the Palace's forces out the front, bolstering its
defences, they practically had free reign of the place. There was only a few stragglers
roaming about that they could easily deal with, and after learning about Akira's ability to
wield multiple Personas, the party set up a balance of getting new Persona and then getting
money.

It was a nice system, but a big flaw in it was that picking fights wherever they could was
sapping their stamina. Ryuji had limits of how much lightning he could throw around, and
Ann's own stamina was taking a toll due to the constant hiding, running, and the general
atmosphere of the Metaverse.

It truly hit home just how dangerous this place could be. Whether you were a Persona-user or
an ordinary person, this dimension would wear you down, one way or another.

Akira watched as Ann gradually shuffled forward, using the left wall of the corridor to move
along. She was clearly pushing herself. Beads of sweat ran down her forehead and if he
looked close, he could see her legs trembling.

He had to say something.

"Ann, it's obvious you're reaching your limit. Maybe we should double back to the Safe
Room so you can take a breather?" He suggested.



"H-Huh?" Ann squeaked. "N-No, you don't need to do that! I'm...I'm fine!" She insisted. But
she wasn't fooling anyone. Even Morgana with his blind infatuation with Ann couldn't ignore
the girl's fatigue.

Akira frowned at her. "Ann..."

The twintailed blonde shook her head defiantly. "I'm fine!" She repeated, taking another step
forward. "I just...I just gotta catch my breath..."

Her hand slid along the wall while her eyes were glued to the floor. That was probably why
she didn't notice what she ended up leaning on until she fell through it.

That being a door.

"Whaaugh?!"

"Lady Ann!"

Ann landed on her rear with a thud and a sharp yelp. Thankfully, the worst that she got as she
fell through the door was a sore butt. She was disoriented, but otherwise unharmed.

Of course, Morgana was quick to scramble to the girl's side with a worried expression on his
round face. "You alright, Lady Ann?" He asked, like a two-foot-tall knight addressing his
queen.

"Y-Yeah, I'm good..." Ann muttered while pushing herself up. "Stupid door...I didn't even turn
the handle..." She grumbled.

"Gotta be more careful." Akira shrugged nonchalantly as he went over to help Ann to her
feet. And yes, he knew he was a smartass. The look Ann gave him as he pulled her to her feet
was funny.

"Th-The shit is this!?"

Ryuji's strangled cry took the pair by surprise. The blond's mask did nothing to hide the
unbridled alarm in his eyes and his mouth was open far enough that they could count all of
his teeth.

And once the pair looked up at the room that Ann had fallen into, they completely understood
Ryuji's reaction.

 

"Kyaaaaa~!"

"Punish me, Kamoshida-sensei~!"

"I can't wait any longer~"

"Put your hands all over me~!"



 

The room that they had just stumbled upon was filled with what could only be described as
pink facsimiles of girls that were wearing nothing but a shirt and bloomers. Lining the walls,
curled up on the floor, all of them moaning and writhing erotically.

 

And not a single one of them looked over 18.

 

Akira felt disgust well up inside him, his gaze darting from one girl to the next. It was
obvious what they were looking at; this was what Kamoshida saw the girls on the volleyball
team as. Nothing but toys, nothing but slaves that were starved for him.

It didn't matter to Kamoshida how well they could spike a volleyball, only that he could
watch their chests bounce as they played. It was revolting. Some of these girls... God, they
barely looked any older than Haruka...

(Arsené went apoplectic within his mask at the mere notion of the roles being reversed...)

 

Ann's flushed skin had quickly turned a pale white, and Ryuji was visibly incensed. How did
every single room they went into only strengthen the notion of how much of a bastard
Kamoshida was?

The group was only snapped out of their funk when a sharp slapping sound rang out from
around the corner, immediately followed by a loud whine.

"You fucking bitch! How dare you flee from your king! You'll pay for such disrespect!"

"Y-Yes King Kamoshida! Forgive me, King Kamoshida!"

The whimpering did not earn the recipient any mercy, as the slap sounded again, followed by
another pained whimper.

What sent shock shooting the spines of the eavesdropping party was that they recognised
those voices. One was obviously the voice of Kamoshida's Shadow, while the other one was
—

"Shiho...!" Ann gasped in horror.

Before the other three could stop her, Ann's sapped energy rushed back to her and she
sprinted through the swathes of girls before them, turning the corner like Hell was on her
heels.

"Ah! That idiot! Ann!" Ryuji hissed. He immediately ran after her, with Akira and Morgana
hot on his tail.



 

One job. She had one fucking job!

 

Upon turning the corner themselves, they found that Ann hadn't gotten far. She was frozen on
the spot, her expression warped into abject horror.

They had found themselves in a room that appeared to be similar to the back of a stage, with
red floor-to-ceiling drapes and candles lining the walls.

And right in the center of that room were two knights standing stalwart over Kamoshida's
Shadow. Next to the vile man was his equally perverted cognition of Ann, as ditzy and
bimbo-fied as before.

And strapped to a standing cross before Kamoshida...it was Shiho. A Cognitive version of
her, of course. She was dressed in the same skimpy attire as Cognitive Ann, save for the fact
that it was bunny-themed all the way down to cottontailed underwear and what could only be
described as a rabbit-eared tiara.

And the bruises...so many bruises marring a young girl who should have never seen this kind
of torment. Fist marks on her belly, grips that had left bruises on her wrists, swollen cheeks, a
black eye and harsh red welts all over. She had been tortured from head to toe and the sight of
Shiho in such a horrid state made Ann feel like she was going to vomit.

It must've been Kamoshida's Shadow venting his frustration at losing Shiho in the real world
to then punish his cognition of her. It was beyond disturbing, especially as Kamoshida raised
his hand and slapped the cognitive Shiho across her already bruised face. "Running off with
that scoundrel like you had any say in the matter... Who the hell do you think you
are!?"

"Like, you're soooo selfish. All King Kamoshida wanted to do was give you his lovely,
lovely love~ But what good are you if you don't surrender to him? Useless. Pathetic. Worse
than garbage." The cognitive version of Ann trilled, practically grinding on Kamoshida like a
dog in heat.

The double's presence furthered Akira's frown. He knew that he shot her in the face the last
time they came here. Cognitions of Palace rulers had a problem with staying down, it would
seem.

"You think you're gonna be shit in the volleyball scene?" Kamoshida snarled, slapping the
restrained Shiho across the face once more. "I'll grind your future into dust, until the most
you can hope for is a job courtside selling peanuts!" Another slap rang across the room.
"Of course, even that would be too good for you..."

"KAMOSHIDA!"



She knew this wasn't the real Shiho. That the real Shiho was back at her place, probably
snacking on the sweets that she had explicitly told her were off-limits. But seeing Kamoshida
spit vitriol at that cognition, demean her with that outfit, strike her like he was the one being
virtuous in teaching her a lesson...

...well you could consider Ann's nerves touched. Her face was pulled into an expression of
absolute fury, her eyes carrying nothing but hatred as she reached for the gun that Akira had
loaned her and pointed it at the foul king, all precautions that she'd been warned about absent
from her mind.

"Ann, wait--!" Akira cried.

 

Ann didn't wait.

 

She pulled the trigger once. There was a bang and a whoosh as the projectile flew past
Kamoshida's ear.

"Guh! The fuck!?" The ruler cursed. He turned around swiftly, and the moment he spotted
them, his face twisted into something even more gruesome. "Y-You peasants again!?"

Ann didn't stop. She kept on pulling the trigger, her teammates' pleas for her to stop lost
under the sound of gunfire and her own fury. However, while her rage was righteous, her aim
left something to be desired. She kept on firing until all that came out of the gun were
chilling clicks. Only three of the bullets had actually hit Kamoshida. And even then, they had
only made the king stagger back a little as if they were nothing more than bug bites.

Kamoshida grunted and wiped his face like the bullets were merely droplets of water. "So
the pests crawl back in...I would have thought you'd have been smart enough to stay
away after you escaped the first time. You're like cockroaches" He snarled. The knights
snapped out of their stupor and snapped into position, standing defensively in front of
Kamoshida.

Ann's chest heaved with anger. Not a single bullet she fired made her feel any calmer.
Kamoshida's nonplussed reaction to them only angered her more.

The teacher's gaze trailed to Akira and his scowl deepened. "You... I haven't forgotten the
humiliation that I suffered at your hands. Don't think you'll be spared recompense. I
will return the agony of what you did to me a hundred—no—a thousandfold!" The king
spat, his golden eyes burning with ire.

Akira cursed under his breath. Dammit, this wasn't supposed to happen! They were just
meant to scout out the Palace for the day and maybe get a feel for what it had in store for
them. Another confrontation with Kamoshida was bad.



After what Akira had done to him last time, Kamoshida wasn't likely to underestimate them
again. For all they knew, he could summon another hundred Shadows out of thin air like
before, and Akira wasn't confident in his ability to deal with them. That power he'd used
against them had left him, and despite trying several times to reach out for it again, it did not
answer the call.

 

As if he wasn't yet worthy of it...

 

He hated to admit it, but retreat seemed to be the best option here. For as long as they were in
the dark about Kamoshida's capabilities, an open confrontation was extremely dangerous.

Even more so with Ann with them who had given away their position, didn't have a Persona
to protect her and had just wasted ALL of her ammo.

 

Seriously, what part of his rules were unclear?!

 

He stepped forward and grabbed Ann's arm. "Ann, back off. Now!"

Ann didn't listen. She shook off Akira's grip and took a step towards Kamoshida. "You evil,
sadistic fucker!" She snarled. "You can waltz around with that perverted version of me all
you want...but how dare...how fucking dare you go after Shiho too!? You don't deserve to
breathe the same air as her!" Ann whipped her hand across her chest.

Kamoshida scoffed. "You have the audacity to question me? I thought I made it clear to
you before. This castle is mine. Everything in it belongs to me. If I wish to savour in its
bounties..." The man grinned at Ann cruelly. "...then what can a whore like you do to stop
me?"

Ann ground her teeth together, but the pair of knights unsheathing their swords with a shrill
ring stopped the girl from advancing. Ann shot a hateful glare between them before looking
past them to Cognitive Shiho. She looked completely defeated. Her face was a mess of
bruises and welts while her eyes had so little life in them that they may as well have been a
doll's.

Kamoshida sneered and forced Cognitive Shiho to look up by yanking on her hair, making
the girl wince mutely. Her faraway gaze met Ann's desperate one, and her expression caused
the blonde's heart to shatter.

"I'll admit, things have gotten a little out of hand thanks to your friend over there."
Kamoshida gestured to Akira who watched on with clenched fists. "But they'll soon get
back on track. Soon as I threaten to expel you, this little bitch will be begging to let me
play with her to save you. What a good friend she is, right?" The man said, tracing his



fingers down Shiho's jawline. "Of course, I'll need to be extra rough with her because of
all the trouble she put me through. But that's what she gets for being friends with a
selfish bimbo like you..."

"She ain't selfish just cause she doesn't want to be your toy! If anyone's the selfish one here,
it's you!" Ryuji accused. He was already brandishing his pipe, and his eyes flickered with a
desire to brain Kamoshida with it.

"Prattling of a broken peasant who tossed his entire future away. Not worth my time."
Kamoshida rolled his eyes and looked back to Shiho.

Ann clenched her fists tightly, with her anger bounding within her blue eyes. "I'm not letting
you lay a finger on Shiho!" Ann growled.

"Oh, and what are you going to do about it, whore?" Kamoshida asked. "You haven't
been able to do anything. Not without that filth over there." He gestured to Akira. "You
didn't want to get in my car, but he bailed you out. You got caught by my knights, so he
needed to save you. You fucked up when you turned down my invitation and put your
precious friend in my sights instead, so he had to deal with it." Kamoshida scowled as he
said that last part. "What happens when you're all alone and have nobody around to save
you? How long do you think that they'll want to burden themselves with you before you
end up being more trouble than you're worth?"

"Hey! Don't you dare talk like that about Lady Ann!" Morgana yowled.

"Yeah! You're the reason that all this shit is happening in the first place!" Ryuji snarled in
tandem.

Ann found herself wilting, just a tiny bit. Because as much as she hated Kamoshida, as much
as he hurt her, as much as she wanted to protect Shiho...

 

She couldn't do anything.

 

All she had done was rely on the guys. Especially Akira. Every ditch, every problem, they
were there to pull her out. In that regard, Kamoshida wasn't exactly wrong.

What would have happened if she'd never met Akira under that awning? Would she have
simply gone along with Kamoshida's demands? Remained trapped in this spiral of blackmail
and unhappiness until she broke? Until she had to give a women's most sacred possession to
an asshole like him? The fact that the answer wasn't a definite 'no' caused her to shudder.

But then...what was she supposed to do? Keep depending on them? Keep being a damsel in
distress forever?

 



"No."

 

"Ah!?" Ann gasped and spun around to look at Akira's stern gaze. Crap, had she said that part
out loud?

"It's not alright that you keep relying on us. Not like this." Akira told the blonde as he shook
his head. "It was our choice to take up this fight, but that doesn't mean your own should end.
You're our friend, but there's only so much we can carry you before you need to walk on your
own two feet." Akira told her, placing a gentle, crimson hand on her shoulder.

"Look at that bastard right now." Akira nodded forward. Ann glanced back at Kamoshida,
only to see the man gleefully ignoring them in favour of toying with Shiho. He fondled the
girl's breasts, dragged his foul tongue up her battered face and made her whimper with every
motion. The spectacle had Ann reeling in horror.

"This was your fight before it was mine, Ann. Are you really alright for it to be taken away
from you? Is that what Shiho is worth to you?"

"O-Of course not!" Ann replied in an offended tone "Shiho's my best friend!"

"Then help her," Akira spoke in a low voice. "Help us." He gave her a small smile. "Let us
rely on you, as you do us. That's what teams are for, right? But before you can stand by us,
you have to be able to stand on your own."

Ann was silent. Her hands shook. Her breath quickened. Her heart pounded against her chest.

Akira was right. Of course he was right.

She looked back at Kamoshida. The repugnant ooze daring to call himself a human was
gripping Shiho's face in a vice grip and was closing in for what could only be the most
stomach-turning of kisses.

She would stand back and let the guys fight this battle without her?

Hell no. Not for a second longer. If she was Shiho's best friend, it was time for her to act like
it!

She turned back to Akira, her face alight with determination. Kamoshida had wronged her for
too long, wronged Shiho for too long and now...

"I'm not gonna let that bastard take anything else away ever again!"

Not Shiho, not Akira, not one more second of her life! But to do that, she needed a lot more
than her grit and a gun with no bullets.

She was slightly confused when Akira's smile twisted into a knowing smirk and he cupped
his chin in wonder. "Huh. So that's what my eyes looked like..."



 

Ann blinked. "Eh?"

 

"My... It's taken far too long."

 

And then the pain began.

 

No more doubt.

 

No more fear.

 

Only FIRE!

Akira hadn't known what a Persona awakening was supposed to look like until now. He'd
been basically unconscious during Ryuji's, Morgana already had his awakened before he met
them and he obviously couldn't observe his own. But if they were as dynamic and explosive
as Ann's was, then he'd certainly missed out.

The room was bathed in fire that burned blue before swirling into a much more conventional
red. Ryuji and Morgana shielded their eyes from the onslaught of light and heat, but Akira
forced himself to watch, to not miss a second.

It had been a bit optimistic, hoping that Ann would awaken to a Persona. There was always a
chance that it wasn't going to work. A gamble that could have gone poorly. But Ann had
defied the odds. She had awakened to that awesome power.

Didn't save her from the ten seconds of agony that an awakening forced upon the recipient
though. It had been kind of uncomfortable to watch Ann roll around and scream like a
banshee before she finally manifested a feline mask that she could pry off.

Kamoshida had tried to interfere of course, ordering his knights to kill Ann before he got
another Persona user to deal with, but Morgana and Ryuji managed to collect themselves and
keep the knights at bay with their slingshot and shotguns respectively. Akira would have
joined in but...y'know, no ammo.

The cat and the blond only needed to shoot a couple of rounds before a tongue of fire lashed
out from the conflagration and turned the pair of knights to cinders. The inferno was so fierce
that not even ashes remained.



"Fuck! Useless knights!" Kamoshida cursed. He swivelled to Cognitive Ann with ire
burning just as bright as Ann's fire. "You! Hurry up and put an end to this, before she--"

A javelin of fire pierced the doppelganger's chest before he could finish the order. The
cognitive blonde wordlessly dissolved into black smoke, leaving Kamoshida on his own.

The pyre seemed wild, leashless, unbound. But there was a deceptive control over it. The fire
was a blade and every cut, every burn would happen at the behest of its Mistress.

 

A heeled boot, red as blood, stepped out of the fire. Not a singe. Not a speck of soot.

 

And from that boot, Ann followed, a face of focused anger hidden behind the feline mask she
now sported. And if the cat-themed motif wasn't obvious enough, the girl fully exhumed
herself from the blaze, showing off a latex catsuit, that was as red as her face was going to be
when she inevitably realised that she was wearing something so...revealing. it was even
complete with pink gloves and a clip-on tail.

That obviously caused reactions with her peers. Ryuji's jaw dropped, Akira was wide-eyed
and Morgana felt like he was falling in love all over again.

 

And Ann? Power surged within her like dam's opened floodgates. Fire obeyed her whims as
if they were her hands, and from those very flames, a new Persona emerged.

 

She towered over Ann, glaring at the pitiful king from behind her own cat-like mask. Her red
and black ballgown fluttered like billowing curtains whilst an ethereal stiletto ground into the
back of a chained, tuxedo-wearing figure with a heart for a head.

 

Yeah, it was weird. But what it certainly wasn't was weak.

 

The Persona flicked her twin-drilled hairstyle behind her head, and the room was ensconced
in columns of unrelenting flames. They formed walls of heat and fire that would incinerate all
that touched it. And Kamoshida sat right in between them, sweating at Ann's mind-boggling
power.

"Kamoshida." Ann spoke in a low voice. "You're the root of all of my misery. I'm never,
NEVER gonna forgive you! I'm not dealing with your bullshit anymore! I will burn down this
entire "kingdom", and you along with it!"



"B-Bitch!" Kamoshida spat at her, not that it did anything. The fire ensured that any of his
knights were completely out of reach, as well as any means of escape. So the cowardly
shadow resorted to scampering behind the astonished Cognitive Shiho.

Ryuji stepped forward. "Seriously, you're using Suzui as a human shield. How 'effing pathetic
can you get?"

"She should be honoured to meet her end protecting her king! It's more than a
rebellious whore like her deser-eeep!" A fireball rushed by Kamoshida's left and impacted
the wall behind him, leaving behind burnt fabric and smouldering, cratered brickwork.

"Shut. Up!" Ann's command came out curt and cold. "You aren't the king of anything right
now. She raised her hand, and her Persona raised her's in turn. "You're nothing but a
disgusting rat, and Carmen and I will take everything away from you!" Fire sprouted to life
upon the hands of both Ann and Carmen, and from that fire, came a crimson bullwhip,
spawning within Ann's hands. She brought it down to the floor, creating a loud crack that
dwarfed even the roaring of her flames.

"Hey, don't count us out!" Ryuji announced. He stepped up behind Ann and took his place at
her side, tapping his pipe against his opposite shoulder. "We all got a bone to pick with this
asshole!" He proclaimed with a smirk.

Morgana hopped over next, brandishing his scimitar with his tiny paws. "I'll fight too! I'll
fight till my last breath for you, Lady Ann! Your battles are my battles!"

Akira chuckled and he also sauntered up to stand beside Ann, forming their four-man team.
He didn't need to say anything. Anyone who thought that he wouldn't fight alongside Ann
must not have been paying attention. Akira bent his knees, raised his fists, and dropped into
Brawler Style, the embroidered dragon burned into his coat glowing with a brilliant blue.

Well, they had come in to scout and now they were about to throw down with a Palace Ruler.
How did that old saying go? 'The best laid plans of mice and men oft go awry'?

 

Kamoshida snarled. These blasted thieves just didn't know when to quit... Well, he'd show
them. He'd show them exactly what happened when they came at the king!

He stepped out from behind Cognitive Shiho – she wouldn't slow these pests down for more
than a second – and he faced the group. "Alright then...you want to come at me? That's
fine. I don't need my knights. I can rip you apart by myself. None of you are leaving
here alive!"

Smoke wafted from his form, and his expression took on a demented grin. The king sucked in
a breath and his naked arms and legs visibly started to bulge.

Akira suddenly got a really bad feeling. Kamoshida was doing something. He could already
feel the shift of power in the air, and the king that he once saw as pathetic was...becoming
something daunting. It was something beyond common thugs, beyond those knights...



Were they ready for this?

 

"LOrD KaMoShIdA! LOrD KaMoShIdA!"

 

And like that, the entire scene paused as a knight clad in golden armour rushed into the room.
It had no face, but panic could be distinguished in its voice. Ryuji blinked at the interruption.
He recognised this guy, or the armour at least "Ain't this the guy from outside?" He asked.

"You! Perfect timing! Hurry, crush these peasants into dust!" Kamoshida bellowed. But
to his and his foes' confusion, the knight sprinted right past the thieves, braving the flames,
and knelt before Kamoshida.

"LOrD KaMoShIdA! wE MuSt gEt yOu tO SaFeTy, ImMeDiAtElY!" It told the king
with urgency bleeding from its distorted voice. "TImE Is rUnNiNg sHoRt!"

"Excuse me?" Kamoshida questioned. Well, yeah, the intruders were a threat, but surely,
once he took on his Asmodeus form...

"My lORd..." The knight raised its head and spoke grimly. "It'S hERE..."

And Akira had never seen a man's face drop from malice to terror so quickly. "Wh-
What...?" Kamoshida squeaked.

And then the wall exploded.

Now, Ann was pretty wound up with the whole awakening thing. Blood rushed through her
veins like magma and every ounce of fire she could muster yearned to burn this foul man to a
crisp.

But as mad as she was, as righteous as she was, there was no fury strong enough for someone
to ignore a GIGANTIC clawed limb busting through the castle walls like they were made of
papier mâché. The sound was deafening, and the entire palace shook as if they were hit with
an earthquake.

"Mrrrooww!?"

"What the shit!? What is that thing!?"

"It's enormous...!"

Various battle cries could be heard from the exterior of the Palace. The ringing of swords and
spears, the screaming that was abruptly cut off after a loud thump. It sounded like a warzone
out there.



Of course, they were all far more concerned about what was happening in here. The massive
black-red claw came down to the floor and made the room shake. The tiled floors gave way
under the weight of the creature and left behind a crater large and deep enough for several
cars to fit inside of it.

The knight was quick to act, scooping up Kamoshida into its arms, who had gone catatonic
from terror. It again ignored the stupefied group and hauled ass from the room, its Lord in its
grasp.

And that was a decision that the thieves were quick to make too. There was no way they
could fight something like that! Not as low level as they were now!

"We...We have to get back to...reality!" Morgana cried. Even that thing's presence made it
hard to breathe. "The...the security level is off the charts! We can't go any fu-further today!"

"No shit!" Ryuji gasped. He then glanced to their recently catsuited ally. "Ann! We gotta go!"

Ann seemed to be trapped in a stupor, but Ryuji's cry broke her out of it. She turned around to
face the guys but another rumble of the castle sent Ann falling on her ass since her legs were
sapped of strength. The consequences of awakening to a Persona were reeling its head at the
worst of times.

She tried to push her body up, but her arms felt no stronger than her legs. She was exhausted.
Carmen had already retreated into her mask to recharge after exhibiting so much power,
leaving Ann completely helpless.

"D-Dammit!" The blonde cursed, feeling more exhaustion coursing through her than she had
ever experienced before. She felt like she was going to pass out. This wasn't good. They
needed to run for their lives and she couldn't even run, period.

More rubble and debris rained down around them as the scaly limb began to retract. A
gradual process that tore through another section of the wall while dust flooded the room.
There were seconds between the removal of each clawed digit as it pulled out, that's how
massive it was.

The room was bathed in the ominous light from outside for only a few moments before it
disappeared once again behind the head of something massive that poked its titanic snout
through the hole it had just made.

And that was when everyone realised what it was they were dealing with.

 

"IS THAT A FUCKING DRAGON!?!"

 

Slitted eyes that were bigger than any one of them were tall blinked at the group that were
little more than flies compared to its gargantuan size. A low growl that resembled that of a
passing jet rung out from its powerful jaws. It was almost enough to deafen them. Its crooked



horns almost reached the ceiling of the room and its grey eyes sat in a pool of red against its
primarily black body.

But the most curious thing about it was the fact that between its horns sat a forest of...frizzy
black hair. What kind of dragon had hair? Much less one that had said hair styled like that of
a human's?

More clashing of metal and futile war cries could be heard. The dragon narrowed its eyes, as
if it were annoyed by such attempts to wound it. And not knowing how it would react meant
that it was well past the time that the thieves should GTFO.

"Ann, come here!" Akira shouted, reaching down and pulling the girl to her feet. It was
already obvious that Ann wasn't going to be able to move, so Akira tossed the girl over his
shoulder like a sack of potatoes. Not as chivalrous as a princess carry, but they didn't have
time to dawdle.

"Gah! H-Hey! What are you--!?"

"Complain after we get out of here!" Akira interrupted. He turned on his heels and sprinted
for the exit, Morgana and Ryuji following close by.

"W-Wait! What about...?" Ann glanced at the Cognitive Shiho. She knew that she wasn't real.
That leaving her behind wouldn't affect the real Shiho at all. But still, it didn't feel right to
leave her behind. Especially in the face of a creature so mighty.

But she didn't exactly have the autonomy, nor the selfishness to act on that desire. Rescuing a
fake Shiho which could possibly get her real friends killed? No, she couldn't ask that of them.
She let her argument die in her throat.

But despite their retreat, despite seemingly leaving her to die...Shiho simply smiled back at
Ann. The cognitive girl didn't have an ounce of dread on her face. And if she was content
with this...

Ann couldn't do anything more.

Best laid plans indeed...

"Hey, Akira?"

"Mmhm?"

"'m really tired."

"I know, Ann."

"Hey, Akira?"

"Mmhm?"



"Can you get me one of those Staminan thingies?"

"No. I'm still pissed off at you that you disobeyed my rules. It worked out, but I'm still pissed.
So suffer."

"Oh..."

...

"Hey, Akira?"

"Mmhm?"

"That dragon was something, huh...?"

"Sure was, Ann."

...

"Hey, Akira?"

"Mmhm?"

"Why are people staring at us...?"

Akira sighed.

 

They had managed to get back to the real world easy enough. The knights and dragon
distracting each other allowed them to slip by unnoticed.

Of course, Ann was still completely drained from her awakening, and returning to the real
world hadn't changed that. The girl looked seconds away from falling asleep on her feet, so
Akira figured that they should do the gentlemanly thing and escort her to the station.

However, Ann could barely move her legs, meaning that Akira had to resort to carrying Ann
piggyback style. And no matter who you were or where you were, carrying a pretty girl on
your back in broad daylight was sure to attract stares.

It didn't help that the awakening had made Ann somewhat delirious with exhaustion, which
was why Ann was constantly asking him stuff.

He could feel Morgana's jealous stare burning holes into him. The not-cat probably would
have given his tail to be in Akira's position right now.

Ryuji was a welcome deviation from the stares and glares, trying to make this scene look like
two friends escorting their third friend and not like a kidnapping. Akira could only imagine
the rumours that would start if any Shujin students saw them like this...



This has certainly been an...informative day. Putting the dragon thing aside (which was
extremely hard to do because again, that dragon business was freaking insane!) they had
learned that Ann had the potential to wield a Persona, and that he had the potential to wield
multiple. Was this what Igor had meant when he called Akira a 'Wild Card?'

There was so much they didn't understand about this whole thing. About Personas, about the
Metaverse and everything in between. And somehow, Akira knew that Igor would gleefully
withhold straight answers. The only reason that they had an inkling of what they were doing
was because of Morgana.

Speaking of which...

"So, Morgana..." Akira looked down at his bag. "Be straight with us. With that dragon there,
what are the chances that we'd be able to make our way through Kamoshida's palace?" He
asked. Even he has to admit, the situation looked grim if they had to work around a beast like
that. Where the hell had it even come from? It wasn't there the last time they entered.

Their Metaverse guide frowned a little, closing his eyes in thought. "I...think it might be
okay...?" He answered.

"Dude, thinking isn't gonna help us. If you're wrong, we're gonna end up as dragon chow
before we can steal Kamoshida's treasure!" Ryuji protested. It was clear he was still shaken
up after coming face to face with something that he'd only seen in fiction.

"No no no, listen!" Morgana interjected "It’s more than likely that that massive dragon was a
part of Kamoshida’s cognition,” Morgana explained. 

Ryuji shot him a skeptical look, crossing his arms. "What? You mean the dragon’s on his
side?" They had no chance if that was the case. The only way they could kill that thing was if
it somehow choked to death when it ate one of them.

Akira adjusted Ann’s weight on his back. No, that didn't sound right... And not because
Kamoshida having a dragon at his beck and call was cheating.

Morgana shook his head once again. "No, just the opposite." He replied "Remember,
Kamoshida was terrified when he heard it was coming. It must be something beyond his
control. I’m pretty sure that it mirrors something in the real world, something that poses a
serious threat to him. Something that he’s afraid of…"

Ann stirred slightly, her voice weak but teasing as she spoke up from Akira’s back.
“Y’know…that dragon had frizzy hair..." She sighed "Sorta like yours, Akira… Frizzy frizzy
frizzy…” 

It was a testament to Akira's reflexes that he didn't trip over a loose stone on the pavement
when a thunderbolt of realisation shot through him and the other two.

"No shitting way..." Akira whispered to himself.



Morgana gasped and his upper body shot out of Akira's bag, his paws raised in a cat's version
of an Eureka pose. "Of course! That makes so much sense!"

Ryuji wasn't exactly caught up yet, but the pieces were clicking into place. "Wait... So are
you saying...?"

Morgana turned to face Ryuji and nodded. His little paw pointed dramatically at Akira. "That
dragon is Kamoshida’s cognition of Akira!"

The silence between them that followed was long, filled only with the sound of chatting
pedestrians and the distant ambience of the train station.

And finally...

"FOR REAL?!" Ryuji squawked. His eyes were wide in excitement, and his lips were pulled
into an eager grin. "You’re sayin' that in the Metaverse, Kamoshida thinks that Akira’s…?"

"A dragon," Morgana declared. "Hellbent on tearing down everything he’s built. Brought
about from when you beat the crap out of him yesterday, most likely." He explained matter-
of-factly.

Ryuji threw his hands in the air, the excitement rushing back into his voice. “Duuuuuude!
That’s so badass! We’ve got a dragon version of you on our side!” 

Akira didn't exactly know what to say. He was still coming to terms with the fact that there
was a dragon version of him within the brain of his PE teacher. He wasn't quite sure how to
feel...

Akira raised an eyebrow at Morgana, who looked less than thrilled by Ryuji’s outburst. The
not-cat sighed. “I mean… we don’t know if it’s on our side..." He commented, looking at the
ground. "Remember, it’s just how Kamoshida sees Akira. He knows we hang out, but he
doesn’t know the specifics. At best, I expect the dragon to leave us alone, or at worst… it
might treat everything in Kamoshida’s palace like it’s a threat, including us.” 

Akira’s grip on Ann shifted a little as he tilted his head in thought. Yeah, Akira could say
with 100% certainty that he didn't wanna meet his end like that, regardless of how cool a
death it would be. “So, better to stay out of… my way then?” 

Morgana nodded, his kitty face serious. “Yeah, that’s probably the smartest thing to do. The
more you stay out of Kamoshida’s way in reality, the less prominent that the dragon version
of yourself in the Metaverse will be.” 

Akira sighed again, this time with a faint hint of resignation. That meant no showing up
outside of Kamoshida's house in the dead of night and scaring the crap out of him.

Damn, when had his life gotten so freaking weird?

...

"Hey, Akira?"



"Mmhm?"

"Does this mean that I'm on the team now?"

Akira couldn't help the sharp chuckle that emerged from his lips.

"Hell yeah, you are." He answered.

Ann responded with a gentle hum. "Akira, you're the best..." She sighed.

And with the shattering of chains, Akira heard the young voice he had heard only once
before. It was but a whisper in the wind, yet every word was clear as day.

 

I am thou...

 

Thou art I...

 

Thou hast acquired a new vow...

 

It shall become the wings of rebellion

 

That breaketh thy chains of captivity.

 

With the birth of The Lovers Persona

 

I have obtained the winds of blessing that

 

shall lead to freedom and new power...

 

Akira smirked.

 



"That's rad."

Despite how astronomically sideways that the simple scouting mission had gone with Ann,
the dragon, and everything in between, everyone unanimously agreed that the Palace wasn't
yet a write-off, including Ann once she managed to overcome her delirium. Regardless of the
danger, there was just as great a threat outside the Palace that they could only permanently
deal with on the inside.

But as it would take a couple of days for the security level within the Palace to normalise,
returning right now was too risky. That was when Morgana had suggested that they use the
two or so days that they couldn't infiltrate the Palace to prep. To stock up on weapons,
medicine, anything they could reasonably get their hands on that would weigh the odds in
their favour.

It was a sensible idea, and thanks to Akira's... proficiency in negotiating with Shadows, they
had a fair amount of cash to work with. It wasn't a crazy amount of money, but it was more
than enough to kit out the whole party and get their hands on some items to stave off the
exhaustion that a Palace crawl would eventually incite.

And both of those encounters were...interesting to say the least.

 

The first was at the local clinic, conveniently just a short walk from Leblanc. Nice to know
that healthcare was always close by. Akira just hoped that they wouldn't ask questions.

According to the guy Akira had asked directions from, this clinic had a reputation of
producing original medicines, some of which could even bestow quote-unquote "unlimited
power."

That didn't exactly bode well. Uncle Kaz told him a story once about a friend who tested a
series of experimental drugs many years ago for cash and that man had gone totally insane...

(Not that the drugs and the insanity were related, mind you.)

Regardless, it was a lead worth following.

 

[Substory 13: The Maverick of Medicine]

 

As it turned out, the clinic was staffed with only one person. A doctor that seemingly took
one look at the notebook for doctoring and scoffed before throwing it out the window. Her
name was Tae Takemi, and Akira's first impressions of her were shaky, to say the least. But
when the doctor actually saw to him, she appeared to be legit. A clean practice and a clear
dedication to her job.



Being a new patient, she gave Akira a few preliminary examinations that any doctor would
do. Height, weight, as well as some auxiliary questions to determine any evident symptoms.

"Have you been suffering any dizziness or discomfort when exerting yourself recently?" She
asked.

"Um, no. I don't think I noticed anything like that. Why do you ask?" Akira questioned.

Tae gestured to Akira's cheekbones. "You just seem to be a little malnourished. Have you
been eating regularly?"

"I...I did miss a couple of days worth of meals a little while ago, but I'm eating regularly
now."

"Hm, well it looks like those signs are starting to recede, so that's good. Still, it's important to
reintroduce food into your system slowly after any sort of fast. You're in pretty good shape,
but if you wanna maintain that, your body needs nutrients."

The doctor wheeled her office chair over to her desk and started scribbling on a piece of
paper. "Even though you're on the mend, I'm prescribing you some nutrient supplements just
in case. Take one of these pills every morning for the next week. And don't pull a hunger
strike again."

Akira would have argued that it wasn't really his choice, but he remained silent and nodded
his head instead. Takemi wasn't exactly...orthodox, but she hadn't given Akira reason to doubt
her capabilities as a doctor. If she truly did dabble in those medicines, they were at least
worth enquiring about.

Akira opened his mouth with that very topic on the tip of his tongue.

"Now then...on to why you're really here..."

...and the clicking of the lock to the only door in the room immediately sent Akira into alert
mode. He looked at the unamused doctor, leaning against the door with crossed arms.
"Um...Doctor Takemi...?" He asked.

"Look, I'm not an idiot. You've been eyeballing this place ever since you got here. You're not
being very subtle, you know." She rolled her eyes. "Let me guess, you're here to scope me
out, right? You're not the first. Won't be the last either." The woman sighed. "All I'm getting
nowadays are customers with ulterior motives..." She muttered to herself.

"Excuse me?" Akira blinked. This woman was used to stickybeaks? That was telling.

"So what was it? Was it a rumour that I could make medicine that could make you strong
enough to wrassle a bull? Or that I could give you a cream to make you look years younger in
seconds? I mean, I do make my own medicines, but those stories are getting way out of
hand..." The woman shook her head.

Akira swallowed and mentally went over his options. If he really needed to, he could force
his way through Takemi and out of the room. That likely wouldn't help his criminal record if



she reported him though, and Akira had a hunch that he shouldn't be so quick to burn bridges.
A local doctor was a useful commodity.

"I...might have heard a thing or two," Akira admitted. "But I truly am here just to buy some
basic medicine. Painkillers, maybe some bandages. Nothing too crazy." Akira shifted a little
on the bed.

Takemi chuckled. "Get into a lot of fights, do you?"

"I live dangerously." Akira answered, neither confirming nor denying that.

"Mm-hm..." Takemi hummed and tapped her bicep with a finger. "Well, I can sell that stuff to
you at least. I don't know what your story is, but it's not my place to pry." She said.

"Hypothetically though..."

"Hm?"

Akira's gaze took on a deep, steely tone. "If there was a medicine like that, one that could
make me stronger or help me to focus...I could really use it." He spoke. His gut told him that
there was credence to the rumours, and if there wasn't such a medicine, then the doctor's
secrecy made no sense.

"Oh? And what would you use it for...?" Takemi asked, hiding no suspicion.

"Nothing bad. That I can promise."

"Well, that's not dubious at all..." Takemi muttered sarcastically. She stopped leaning against
the door but didn't move from her spot. "Medicine like that wouldn't come cheap. It'd cost as
much as a car. At minimum. Top-shelf stuff would be worth way, way more. Not something
that you can afford. So you should probably just give it up. Makes it easier on all of us." She
advised. It confirmed that there was indeed a special kind of medicine that she had on hand.
She had abandoned all pretences.

And the price tag was definitely a problem. The Metaverse was bountiful, but it'd take a
while to scrounge up that kind of money. And Akira was hoping to save where he could.
Lawsuit and everything...

What was the play here?

 

- Turn on the charm

 

- Offer to work for it

 



- Essence of Shameless Beggar

 

The second option seemed the most feasible

"I...could pay in favours. I can deal with manual labour, even security if you need me to."
Akira offered. He was grasping at straws here, but he didn't have much of a choice.

To his surprise, Takemi took on a contemplative expression. "Security? You mean setting up
cameras or motion detection? This place isn't exactly prone to being robbed. It's just a back
alley clinic." She replied.

"I was thinking...more like a bouncer?" Akira corrected. "You said you get lots of patients
with ulterior motives, right? I'm good at reading people, and if you get someone you'd rather
not deal with, I can escort them out." Akira said. "I can handle a hooligan or two."

Takemi seemed to stare at him harder but Akira refused to back down. There was nothing
else he could offer. There did seem to be gears turning in the woman's head, and Akira
somehow knew he wasn't going to like what happened when they made a full rotation...

"You are in pretty good shape for someone your age..." Takemi commented. "And I'll admit,
there is something that I've been working on that I could use your help with. You're a pretty
good candidate too. Far better than anybody I could find on my own." She added.

That was foreboding, but Akira leaned forward. "What do I have to do?" He asked.

Takemi finally stepped away from the door and held up a finger. "Wait here for a second."
She walked past Akira and behind a medical privacy screen, where Akira could hear the hum
of a small refrigerator being opened and the clinking of glassware.

He would be lying if he said he wasn't unsettled. And an anxious blue eye staring up at him
from his bag indicated that he wasn't the only one.

Takemi soon returned, carrying a small beaker filled with red liquid. Akira made to ask what
it was, but found said beaker in his hand before he could open his mouth. The chill of
refrigerated glass sent shivers up his arm. "What's this?" He asked.

"The first of hopefully many concoctions I'll have you drink," Takemi answered. "You see,
I'm trying to perfect a unique medicine, but I need someone to undergo some clinical trials.
An able-bodied young man like you is the perfect guinea pig~"

Takemi chuckled.

Akira glanced down at the crimson liquid, and his reflection stared back at him. For all he
knew, this could be poison or it could be fruit juice. It did have a vaguely antiseptic scent to
it, like cold medicine. "So...I drink this, and you'll hook me up with your special medicine?"
Akira questioned.



"I suppose I could be persuaded..." Takemi replied. She went over to her desk to grab a
clipboard. "...but if you're having second thoughts, you're free to leave." She turned back to
him. "After all, drinking some mystery medicine that a shady doctor gave you is
beyond...stu...pid..."

Takemi looked at Akira.

 

Then to the empty beaker.

 

Akira looked at her.

 

She looked back at him.

 

The clinic was silent for about five seconds.

 

"..."

 

"..."

 

"..."

 

"..."

 

"...I think I'm gonna hurl..."

 

Takemi handed him a vomit bag.

In the end, the clinic worked out. After Akira had stopped spewing, and Takemi had gotten
some data, they went into officiating their deal. Akira was prohibited from telling anyone



about the medicine or the clinical trials, and in exchange for his participation, Takemi would
sell him basic medical items, as well as offer good prices for her special medicine.

Akira left the clinic that day with a bunch of medical supplies, a couple of experimental
brews, the Death Arcana, and both Takemi and Morgana's assertion that he was, in fact, an
idiot.

The following, and arguably more interesting encounter came the following day and involved
them deciding to up their arsenal. Morgana had expressed a keenness in bolstering their
strength through more refined weapons and firearms.

Luckily, Ryuji happened to know a place in one of the back alleys of Shibuya.

 

Yes. Another back alley.
